## Wave 112 integration (cars second pass, landscape dressing, waterfalls and airstrips, outskirts towns and the 9 km world, hero look v3, two critics)
- Full suite (`tools/test_all.sh`, 3 jobs, clean user dir through `XDG_DATA_HOME`, 256 tests): 247 pass, 7 FLAKY, 2 first-run FAIL, both fixed below. After the fixes the tests of the touched areas (terrain_world, release_prep, interior_gameplay x3, landscape_dressing, outskirts_settlements, water_world, world x5) ran again: 13 of 13 pass.
- FLAKY by name (pass on retry, no threshold changed): `test_interior_gameplay` (node count, made robust below), `test_perf_budget` ("...and back within range it is drawn again"), `test_shooting_accuracy` (zone hits empty under load, known), `test_slice_life` (tier 1: 6+ cars moving at 3 of 5 spots), `test_traffic_police_feel` (spike strip at heat 3.5, known), `test_vehicle_overlap` (one 0.33 s overlap against the 0.3 s limit: live-traffic statistics), `test_world_missions_air` (the air race counted two ladder missions: an automatic event finished in the same window).
- Fixed by the integrator:
  - `test_release_prep` (ORM masks import at 1K): the new `assets/textures/hero_suit_orm.jpg.import` had `process/size_limit=0`. The atlas is 1024 px so it changes no texel, but the size-tuning convention (`tools/release/tune_imports.py`) and the test want the line; set to 1024.
  - `test_terrain_world` (far tiles): the 9 km world has 96 far tiles and two are built at a time (about 1.3 s each), so the fixed 45 s wait failed even on an idle machine (68 of 96). `TerrainWorld.far_jobs_max` (default 2, the phone behaviour is unchanged) is new; the test raises it to 4 and waits on the counter (`_wait_progress`: ends when all 96 are built, fails when no tile finished for 20 s or after 110 s of real time). All 96 are built in 13 s; the meaning of the checks (all built, fewer than 100, 20k+ triangles) is the same.
  - `test_interior_gameplay` ("repeated enter / exit does not leak nodes", limit 12): the count moved by +0 or +42 on identical runs because the car streamer (one car = 23 nodes) and the outskirts sites (26 colliders unloaded while the hero is inside, rebuilt when he comes out) change it on the clock. A probe showed the interior itself is clean (each enter adds about 45 nodes, each leave removes them). Both streamers now rest while the check counts; 4 of 4 runs pass (+0 to +2); the limit of 12 is untouched.
- Found, NOT fixed (none of it is from this wave; each passes, so CI stays green):
  - `test_vehicle_roster` stops at its line 94 (`NearTraffic.instance.process_mode = ...`: NearTraffic is a RefCounted since cars v2, wave 111), so only the data checks run and everything from the ground vehicles on is skipped while the test says PASS. With the line replaced by `NearTraffic.instance.count = 0` and `reset()` (as `test_big_city` does) 79 checks pass and 4 FAIL: `tank: rolls` (4 - 8 m in 14 s, needs more than 8), `plane: lands and brakes to a stop without wrecking` (hp -53 of 420, hits 22, landings 0), `plane: the hero gets out`, and `the tank readout names the cannon and its shells` (stale: the readout has two rows now, cannon and coax gun, the test wants exactly one). The same four fail with `QT_NO_LANDSCAPE=1 QT_NO_VEGETATION=1`, so they are not the landscape. I put the test back as it was: wave 113 (tank, vehicle damage) and wave 116 (planes) should fix the line and these four checks together.
  - `test_audio` logs a script error from `_simulate` (tests/test_audio.gd:90: `Sfx.variants_of(...)[0] as AudioStreamWAV` is null for one sound of the firefight mix; the function returns and the test goes on) and `test_campaign_runner` one at :980 (`_button(&"deploy")["rect"]` after the test closed the MORE page, so the NEW GAME button is not in the layout; the last checks of `_chapter_select_ui` are skipped). Neither area was touched by this wave.
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, nothing else running, stale `roam_world.cfg` removed first, final tree incl. the fixes above): LOW OK (72 draws, 94 total, 134.6 k triangles, tex 110 MB, death-spike 108 draws), MED OK (97, 129 total, 272.6 k, tex 141 MB, death 144), HIGH OK (113, 149 total, 378.8 k, tex 207 MB, death 167). Texture limits (200 / 260 / 360 MB) and the budgets are untouched; the numbers equal the builders' last runs.
- `tools/full_bot.sh`: PASS (verdict PASS, 7.9 simulated minutes, 0 errors; 21 headless dummy-renderer messages ignored; census growth is wildlife, HUD touch controls and one streamed car).
- `tools/visual_qa.sh`: 27 / 27 pass. `docs/shots/latest.png` looked at, defects: (1) the street frames are wide empty plazas at night with a small hero, no traffic in three of four views and the QUANTUM CORES 0 / 6 banner on every free frame; (2) the boss frame still shows CONTRACT FAILED ABANDONED over the objective line; (3) in the hero frame a yellow bus fills the left third; (4) the car frame's coupe is a flat violet-pink; (5) the title backdrop is very dark; (6) NEW: from behind the hero's cloak reads as a stiff grey panel left of the body in the street frames (the folded cloth shows only from the sides). The SUIT screen frame (live turntable hero, six suit cards, schemes, accents, person row) is clean and fits. None of these was a small fix. ## Wave 113: weapon roster (builder w113-weapons-roster, two agents; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Weapon roster v3"; progress log docs/sprint/progress/w113-weapons-roster.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/weapons_ui_before_after.jpg, weapon_wheel_rings.jpg, weapons_v3_in_hand.jpg)
- Done: 125 NEW weapons with original names (old 58 + 125 = 183; with the blast builder's pack 229 and more). Families, old + new: pistol 15, smg 12, assault 14, battle 7, carbine 7, dmr 8, sniper 11, shotgun 12, lmg 7, rotary 5, bow 6, flamer 5, melee 28, energy 17, quantum 12, exotic 6 (every target of the brief is met or beaten); wheel classes pistol 15, smg 12, rifle 30, shotgun 12, sniper 24, heavy 20, melee 28, quantum 12, energy 23. Every weapon is a data row (`weapon_rows_v3.gd`, damage worked out from `power` so the balance band holds) with its own mechanic (about 30 perks: opener, final round, ramp, knock, slow, mark, shock, chill, dissolve, ricochet, pierce, refund, heal, steady aim, crit blast, pull, cone, mist ...), a second fire mode of its own (nine new alt kinds), ammo types (7), a 3D model, a small and a large picture, a class sound set with 3 variation layers (16 sets, 84 OGG), a place on the tier ladder (28 crates incl. secret caches at outskirts places, enemy drops, shops, mission rewards by the ladder), HERO MODE (`WeaponDefs.hero_mode`).
- Five attachment slots (OPTIC, MUZZLE, GRIP, MAGAZINE, LASER) with 21 parts and per-weapon fit rules; a loadout with class chips, pages of eight, a star (FAVOURITES) and RECENT tabs, slot tabs, a FITTED line; a catalogue with class chips and nine tiles a page; a two-level weapon wheel (12 discs: classes + RECENT + FAVS, then a ring of the class); an AMMO button behind MORE (ammo types had only a keyboard key); the ARMS DEALER back room and random weapon drops by hero tier (`weapon_shops_v3.gd`); secret caches name their place.
- Files: new `scripts/player/{weapon_rows_v3,weapon_defs_v3,weapon_mech,weapon_models_v3,attachment_parts_v3,weapon_shops_v3}.gd`, `scripts/ui/weapon_browser.gd`, `scripts/common/strings_weapons_v3.gd`, `tools/audio/make_weapons_v3.py`, assets `audio/shot_v3_*`, `audio/wlayer_*`, `icons/w_*`, `icons/wl_*`, tests `test_weapons_v3`, `test_weapon_balance_v3`, `test_weapon_ui_v3`, bot `bot_weapon_ui`; changed (hook lines, keep them when those files are rewritten): `weapon_defs.gd` (PACKS and merged tables), `player_weapon.gd` (`mech.` calls, `_tick_heat` fix, AMMO toast), `attachments.gd`, `weapon_ladder.gd`, `weapon_crates.gd`, `weapon_roster.gd` (headline), `economy.gd`, `loadout_screen.gd`, `weapons_screen.gd`, `weapon_wheel.gd`, `radial_wheel.gd`, `touch_controls.gd` (ring, badges, AMMO button), `hud.gd` (routes `wheelcls_`, `fav_toggle`, `slot_`, `wcls_`), `progress.gd` (favourites and recent in the save), `strings.gd`, `interior_data.gd`, `tools/icons/icon_maker.gd`.
- Tests: `test_weapons_v3` (135 checks, 12 s, real player scene: every one of the 125 weapons equips, fires, reloads, uses its alt-fire and damages a dummy; 49 status keys land; opener, ramp, refund, pierce, ammo types, attachments, wheel, shops, drops, save and load), `test_weapon_balance_v3` (TTK table by class, family and range; no outlier above 3x the class median; EASY kill within 2.5 s, HARD within 7 s), `test_weapon_ui_v3` (main scene, 93 checks: loadout, catalogue with BUY, the wheel through real touch events, 30 layout scans at 844x390, 1200x540 and 1280x720). Bugs they found and I fixed: a spin-up weapon without heat keys crashed `_tick_heat`, GRAVITY GUN had `knock` 0, a static initialiser ran before WeaponDefs existed. Moved with a reason (the old assumptions of the first rosters): test_weapon_roster, test_weapon_cards (new pages), test_weapons_depth, test_energy_destructive_weapons, test_director_round2_d (slot tabs), test_director_round3_b (geometry), test_weapons_available_display (class wedges), test_hud (the AMMO button sits in the outer MORE column), test_interior_gameplay_vendors (the dealer's back room opens at tier 2), test_audio_real_samples (budget). A batch of 38 touched tests passed; `test_interior_gameplay` "repeated enter / exit does not leak nodes" is the known flaky one (+38 .. +50 against 12, passes on about half of the runs: streamers of other builders).
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh`, final tree): LOW OK (96 total draws, 138.4 k triangles, tex 110 MB, death 108), MED OK (129, 271.3 k, 141 MB, death 145), HIGH OK (149, 377.5 k, 207 MB, death 167). The first run was MED OVER (281.7 k): the 28 new crates made the crate MultiMesh draw 45 crates (7 k triangles) all over the map in every view; `WeaponCrates` now draws only the crates that are ready, known and within 320 m (compacted, `visible_instance_count`), which pays for the roster and took LOW / MED / HIGH 1.5 - 3.5 k under the wave 112 numbers. Texture limits untouched (the icons load only while a menu shows them).
- Added weight: scripts 250 KB, 84 OGG 662 KB, 250 icons 625 KB (small 278 KB, large 347 KB), no model file (the weapons are meshes built at run time), 0.8 MB of contact sheets (not exported): about 1.6 MB in the repository and the APK.
- Found, not fixed (other builders' files): the gear card text of the C4 ran off its card (blast builder's new line; I made the card wrap long text); the weapon pop plate was not visible in a frame 8 frames after a switch (not investigated); the objective line shows through the open wheel's scrim under its caption (`touch_controls.gd`, pre-existing).
- Gaps: the 125 models are generated boxes and prisms (the weapon art wave replaces them: `WeaponModels.mesh(id)` is the one entry point, and a long melee weapon is held like a gun in the viewer's `aim` pose); names and role lines of the roster stay English in es / hi (UI words, slot names and the 12 new parts are translated); the catalogue has no star; weapons of the blast pack come last on the mission ladder (the roster's 125 first, by tier); nothing measured on a phone.

## Wave 113: enemy roster look v3 (builder w113-enemy-look, three agents over two restarts; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Enemy look v3"; progress log docs/sprint/progress/w113-enemy-look.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/enemy_look_*.jpg)

The five roster-2 mercenaries and the city troopers (`HumanoidVariants.CLASS_LOOK`) wear the new bodies; 17 new species rows and 13 new encounters (auth_patrol ... grid_assault) are in the roster and the director.
- Second pass (this restart): floating HEALTH PIPS for every damaged roster enemy and THREAT MARKERS for aware ones 30-95 m away (`FoePips`, one MultiMesh, 72 instances, one draw call only while something is hurt or far and aware); telegraph lines were screen-wide translucent sheets near the camera (`AlienFx._place_line`: 0.9 degrees of view at most, fade at the lens) and bolts are darts now; the creature wind-up flash lights an outline and the glowing parts instead of washing the body salmon; angular armour plates for brute, elite and hunter; lighter paints, a readability floor and a distance lift in `foe_body.gdshader` (the dark authority armour was black in rain and overcast); LOD ranges by tier (LOW: mid mesh from 11.5 m, far from 33 m); the rig of far bodies runs every 2nd (26 m) and 3rd (50 m) frame (crowds of 8+); a loading-time warm-up (two hook lines in `scripts/common/shader_warmup.gd`).
- Files: new `scripts/roster/{foe_kit,foe_arms,foe_looks,foe_human,foe_species,roster_human,foe_pips}.gd`, `assets/shaders/{foe_body,foe_creature,foe_pips}.gdshader`, `assets/models/enemy_kit.glb` (1.4 MB, 376 parts) and `assets/models/foes/*.glb` (9 bodies, 2.7 MB), `tools/blender/{enemykit,creaturekit}/`, `tools/blender/{model_enemy_kit,model_foes}.py` (`.venv-tools/bin/python tools/blender/model_foes.py [id ...]`), look-dev `tools/foe_view.sh` + `tests/foe_viewer.gd` + `tests/bot_foe_look.gd` (`hurt=`, `keep=1`, `kill=`, `FOE_OLD_LOOK=1`), test `tests/test_enemy_look_v3.gd`; hooks in `characters/hero_character.gd` (is_foe paths), `characters/humanoid_variants.gd`, `roster/{roster_species,roster_director,roster_kit,roster_foe}.gd`, `aliens/{alien_species,alien_creature,alien_fx}.gd`, `scripts/common/shader_warmup.gd` (keep these hook lines when those files are rewritten).
- Tests: `test_enemy_look_v3` (95 checks, 50 s, real scene: 13 looks and 9 creatures load, budgets per LOD, no black patches, silhouette metrics, weapon by id, 22 types spawn and behave in the roster, pips, markers, thin lines, tier LODs, throttled rigs, warm-up, the real game with real encounters), plus `test_alien_world`, `test_enemy_roster2`, `test_humanoid_variants`, `test_humanoid_wiring`, `test_real_fight`, `test_enemy_look`, `test_damage_everywhere` PASS after the second pass.
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, tree of the second pass): LOW OK (96 total draws, 139.0 k triangles, tex 110 MB, death 107), MED OK (129, 272.1 k, 141 MB, death 149), HIGH OK (149, 378.0 k, 207 MB, death 170 of 170). The first gate of the second pass was HIGH OVER (death 171 > 170) and I found why: the EMPTY pip MultiMesh still cost one draw call in every tier; `FoePips` now hides its node while nothing is hurt or far and aware (HIGH 150 -> 149 total, death 171 -> 170; LOW and MED lost one too). With the kit troopers switched off (`FOE_NO_KIT=1`, the old hero-shader look) HIGH measured the same 171, so the kit bodies cost no draw; HIGH death spike has zero headroom (170 of 170 after the damage builder's 170, wave 111 had 170): the next builder to add one draw to a death turns it OVER.

- Gaps: no MultiMesh impostors for far crowds (a far human is one skinned draw, caps 4 / 6 / 9; the rig CPU is throttled instead); the riot shield cannot break off; creature faces and hands are simple; at 40-80 m a figure is 20-45 px tall on a phone (camera distance, not this wave): the markers and pips carry the read; the roster humans keep the carbine models of the weapon registry until wave 114; no es / hi strings for the new first-contact lines; nothing measured on a phone.

## Wave 113: independent critic, LOOK lens (critic-fixer w113-critic-look; about 700 phone-aspect frames at LOW and MED, every sheet opened; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Wave 113 critic, LOOK lens"; log docs/sprint/progress/w113-critic-look.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/critic113_look_{status,glass_fire_scorch,debris_radius,blasts_low,blasts_med,enemies,animals,deaths}.jpg)
- Fixed (each verified in before / after frames; checks in `tests/test_critic_look_w113.gd`, real scene, about 45 checks): (1) burning, frozen and shocked bodies were tiger stripes, zebra stripes and a flat silhouette, and the aliens showed none of the three: one include `assets/shaders/dmg_status.gdshaderinc` for `hero_body`, `foe_body` and `foe_creature` (position on the body, never the UV; `FoeFx._shader` sets the creature's own material); (2) the windscreen of every blasted car was black and white noise 8 m away: mip levels on the crack texture, blur by distance, frost when shattered (`car_glass.gdshader`, `VehicleVisual._cracks_texture`); (3) fire hung in the air as flat teardrops: tongues are anchored to the street (`BlastFx.ground_under`), a low bed under them, licks that climb and a red tip (`blast_cloud.gdshader`); (4) the fresh scorch on a shop front was a solid cream starburst: a short ring of glints (`fx_marks.gdshader`); (5) torn car panels were black planks and the flung wheels black balls: `car_debris_panel.gdshader` and a lit tyre on a private mesh copy (`CarDebris`); (6) the radius ring of a grenade, mine or strike vanished on a bright or orange street (additive, fading wall): alpha band with a dark skirt and crawling dashes (`blast_wall.gdshader`); (7) a mine or a charge looked like a grenade: a dirt plume from the ground (`BlastFx._extras`); (8) the six contract mercenaries were one black cut-out at 15 m against the sun: a fake ambient of the paint in `foe_body.gdshader`.

- Not fixed (owners): the strike ring at 34 m is still a thin line and the aim label touches the equip card for the first seconds (hud / blast_preview); grenade, rocket and mortar are one fireball family (the table has the recipes `dust`, `dome`, `shock` waiting); humans at 40 m are dots (camera); a blank white rectangle hangs above the street at the look-dev spot (-47.6, -2.9) in the damage frames (an unlit billboard?); `alien.gdshader` (old aliens, not the roster) has no status look; wolves of a herd scene reach the hero 3 s after a blast (behaviour, fine); `test_real_fight` had one flaky red on a full machine (longest cover 15.8 s against 12 s), green on the re-run.
- Look-dev bots (all need `tools/bot.sh`, `--fixed-fps 12`): `tests/bot_critic_blasts.gd` (the blast arsenal bot plus a registered entry), `bot_critic_foes.gd` (rows, statuses and kills of operatives, roster humans, aliens), `bot_critic_crowd.gd` (crowd alive), `bot_critic_animals.gd` (a herd and a blast in a forest clearing).


## Wave 113 integration (weapon roster 200+, blast-radius arsenal, damage everywhere, enemy roster look v3, two critics)
- What the wave holds (the blast and damage builders wrote no section of their own, so their summary is here; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Blast arsenal" and "Damage everywhere", logs docs/sprint/progress/w113-weapons-blast.md and w113-damage.md): BLAST ARSENAL = `BlastTable` (`scripts/combat/{blast_table,blast_rows,blast_ops,blast_fx,blast_preview,blast_card,world_blasters}.gd`): 65 catalogue entries (grenades 12, launchers 14, mines and charges 10, strikes 10, vehicle and aircraft ordnance 9, hero ultimates 6, world blasters 4) plus 15 migrated legacy entries (old numbers unchanged) and 46 hero weapons (`gr_*`, `la_*`, `mn_*`, `st_*`, wheel class BLAST); `Game.explode` returns `BlastTable.explode(...)` (a Dictionary); flat / linear / quad falloff measured at 0.5 / 1.0 / 1.5 x radius, cover, knockback and lift, prop chains, cars toss / flip / burn, civilians and animals flee, statuses; previews of the TRUE radius (grenade arc and ring, launcher ring, mine ring, strike footprint on the ground and the minimap, label "NAME  RADIUS 6 M", a diagram on the weapon card); ultimates on key O and the ULT button behind MORE; barrels, gas tanks and fuel trucks as world blasters. DAMAGE EVERYWHERE = `DamageWorld` (one line in `main.gd`) plus `StructureDamage` (broken windows, spall, wall breaches and craters in 3 m cells), bullet holes by material (`fx_decals`), prop wear stages and debris (`Breakables.fling`, broken props are no cover), fire spreading between cars and wooden props, people (`FoeFx`: hit zones, armour plates at 30 % and 62 %, faction-coloured splatter, burn / frozen / shock / dissolve statuses, deaths by cause) and cars (`CarFx`: bullet holes, glass cracks then shatters, flat tyres, hood pop, scorch, burning wrecks); `QT_DMG=0` switches the new layers off. Their gaps are in `reports_w113.md` (no ragdolls, no liquid on car bodies, facades are decals plus debris, nothing measured on a phone).
- Full suite (`tools/test_all.sh`, 3 jobs, clean user dir through `XDG_DATA_HOME`, 265 tests, 45.7 min): first run 252 pass, 3 FLAKY, 10 FAIL. All ten were looked at one by one, each against the wave 112 tree (a copy of HEAD in the scratchpad) so that environment and wave could be told apart. After the fixes below the ten red tests, the three flaky ones and the tests the fixes touch (about 40 files) ran again alone and in batches of three jobs: all PASS in the last batches (`test_soak_systems`, `test_soak_quick` and `test_full_quick` together: 3 PASS). The whole suite was not run a second time (45 minutes).
- Fixed by the integrator (stale test, environment or real bug; no threshold was loosened without a reason):
  - REAL BUG, `bool(en.get(&"aware"))` on enemies that have no `aware` (aliens and roster creatures; only `Drone` has it): "Invalid call. Nonexistent 'bool' constructor" every tick in smoke and blind zones (`BlastOps._tick_smoke` / `_tick_blind`; the same pattern in the older `Throwables` smoke, flash and lure code): `test_full_quick` failed twice (full bot, `weapons` activity, `st_air_l`). Now `en.get(&"aware") == true`.
  - REAL FINDING, `test_soak_systems` orphans (heist and arena probes +18 .. +24, limit 14): every `FoeHuman` prototype (one per look x variant x weapon, the source of each duplicate) is a body tree that never enters the scene tree, so `Performance` counts 9 orphan nodes at the first spawn of each look: a bounded cache, not a leak. `FoeHuman.cached_node_count()` counts it and `tests/soak_lib.gd` `monitors()` subtracts it, so the check still catches a lost body (ARCHITECTURE "Enemy look v3"). The remaining +6 steps of the heist start and of the wanted rounds are older (the wave 112 tree shows them too).
  - `test_soak_quick` (FLAKY in the first batches, 2 of 3 solo runs red on this tree, 3 of 3 green on the wave 112 tree): the first blast of a session builds the blast and damage pools (BlastOps, BlastFx, marks, debris, DamageWorld, pips), which showed up as +350 nodes between checkpoint 0 and 1 against the 8 % limit. `tests/bot_soak.gd` `warm_pools()` now blasts three operatives once before the baseline checkpoint (the same warm-up idea as the enemy types and car kinds); growth is +130 .. +230 now, 3 of 3 green. Limits unchanged.
  - `test_audio_budget2`, `test_audio_redesign`: the audio folder is 24.5 MB now (the roster's 84 OGG weapon sounds, 0.66 MB) against a 24 MB budget: 25 MB in both, with the reason, as the roster builder did in `test_audio_real_samples` (`docs/AUDIO_SOURCES.md` follows).
  - `test_energy_destructive_scene`: the first level of the two-level weapon wheel is ten class wedges plus the RECENT / FAVOURITES tabs; the check counts ten class wedges (type `class`), at most twelve discs, all on the screen (it said ten discs).
  - `test_hud_camera_final`: the ULT and AMMO buttons (on foot, behind MORE) share the corner of BACK (driving, behind MORE); they can never be on screen together (`more_ult` / `more_ammo` need `not _driving()`, `drive_more` needs `_driving()`): added to `EXCLUSIVE`, like the other pairs.
  - `test_stability`: (1) the wreck's blast now shoves the hero (BlastTable knockback and lift), so the check "stands on the ground" waits for the landing (4 s at most); (2) a blast alone never takes the hero below 25 % (NORMAL) or 40 % (EASY) of his health (`BlastTable._soften`, "no sudden deaths", docs/DESIGN.md), so the weakened-driver death flow runs on HARD (restored after).
  - `test_character_motion`: the muzzle flash lives 0.055 s of REAL time, two physics frames outlast it on a loaded machine: the three cards are counted in the same frame.
  - `test_perf_budget` ("...and back within range it is drawn again"): fails on the wave 112 tree in this sandbox as well (four physics frames can run inside one process frame): the same explicit `r._process(0.0)` tick as the check above it. `test_ai_tactics2` (68 s here) and `test_soak_systems` (75 s) time out at the default 60 s on the wave 112 tree too: `TIMEOUT_SECONDS := 140`.
- FLAKY by name (pass on retry, nothing changed): first run `test_director_round2_c` (hurt engine wobble), `test_mission_board` (stuck findings 3 against 2), `test_world_missions_air` (ladder credit 0 against 2), and `test_soak_quick` until its warm-up fix above; `test_mission_audit` failed both attempts under three jobs (chase targets escaped, guide recoveries) and passes alone (72 s) and in a batch: load sensitive, it is the test that guards "tasks get stuck".
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, nothing else running, final tree): LOW OK (94 total draws, 133.1 k triangles, tex 110 MB, death 107 of 115), MED OK (129, 272.0 k, 141 MB, death 149 of 150), HIGH OK (149, 380.4 k, 207 MB, death 169 of 170); run on the final tree (the first run before the last small script fixes gave the same numbers, LOW 93 draws). No limit was touched. MED and HIGH have no headroom left in the death spike: the next builder who adds one draw to a death turns it OVER. CPU, because the gate does not measure it: `tools/cpu_frame.sh` (headless, 240 frames, 4 enemies) LOW avg frame 14.3 ms (wave 112: 15.5), script total 7.50 ms (7.06), MED 17.4 ms (15.8), script 8.54 ms (8.28): about +5 % in a steady scene. The staged view of `perf_report` (the first spawn of each look is inside its window) shows LOW script ms ENEMY AI 2.26 against 1.12 and PLAYER 2.17 against 1.71 (total 7.6 against 5.9, +29 %): the kit bodies cost CPU at their first spawn and about 0.5 ms per near human per frame (desktop numbers, a phone is 3 to 4 x slower).
- `tools/full_bot.sh` skipped by the rule (it runs every second wave, the last run was wave 112); `test_full_quick` (soak plus monkey) passes. `tools/visual_qa.sh`: 27 / 27 pass. `docs/shots/latest.png` looked at, defects: (1) the 27 spots show none of this wave's content (no roster enemy, no blast, no weapon wheel): add spots for an enemy lineup at 15 m, a blast in progress, the wheel and loadout with 229 weapons, a town in the outskirts; (2) the four street frames are wide empty night plazas, a small hero, one or no cars (street_nw: a blown-out cream box van with one window patch, chase: a yellow smooth-shaded van fills a third of the frame, both read as toys beside the new sedans); (3) QUANTUM CORES 0 / 6 NEAREST 1117 M is on every free frame and stacks with "6 ENEMIES DOWN" and the objective line (boss: CONTRACT FAILED ABANDONED over the objective); (4) from behind the hero's cloak is a stiff grey slab left of the pack; (5) the ability row, the weapon line and the ammo box fill the lower centre over the hero's legs (the HIJACK SEDAN prompt too). Nothing here was a small fix.
- Added weight of the wave (staged commit): game assets about 6.1 MB with their .import files (models 4.10 MB: `enemy_kit.glb` 1.4 and nine foe GLBs 2.7; icons 342 PNG 0.88 MB; 84 OGG 0.68 MB; shaders 42 KB; .import files 0.37 MB), about 20 k lines of scripts, tests and tools, 12 MB of contact sheets and 28 refreshed visual QA PNGs in docs/shots (not exported), no raw archive, no third-party asset. `tools/size_report.sh`: assets total 305 MB (297 MB at wave 112); an APK grows by about 4 MB (the GLBs import to 1.6 MB, the icons VRAM-compress); no export was made here. Test time: 9 new test files (about 4 min together); estimated CI shards from the per-test times of this sandbox divided by its measured speed against the wave 112 CI run (about 1.4 x slower): 26 / 22 / 23 / 19 minutes against the 30 minute job limit, so the next wave must not add more than about 3 minutes of tests to shard 0 (or the limit in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` goes up).
- Tidiness: `tests/dev_w113_carshots.gd` (named nowhere) moved out of the repo; `dev_w113_{sweep,engage,spam,firstspawn}.gd` stay (named in the play critic's log); no `zz_*` or `*_tmp` file in the tree; the generated digests `docs/sprint/progress/auto_*.md` and `AUTO_INDEX.md` are not committed (as in wave 112); my baseline copy of the wave 112 tree and all logs in the scratchpad deleted; disk 13 GB free after the run.
- Open follow-ups (for wave 114 and the performance recovery of wave 119): (1) LOW draw peaks during blasts, +7 .. +18 per blast (a frag among four enemies 93 -> 106, a gas tank 106 -> 124): on a busy street 8 of 13 blasts pass the death ceiling 115; needs per-tier caps of the pooled `CPUParticles3D` (`FX.burst`) and `ExplosionPool`; (2) CPU of the kit bodies: no MultiMesh impostors for far crowds, rigs are only throttled; warm all 13 looks (the loading warm-up covers 8); (3) a flat time-to-kill (about 2 s in every class) and EMP / BLIND / STUN reach humans although the cards say machines; (4) `BlastOps.shared` orphans a node when its parent is busy (headless harness only); (5) blasts can no longer kill the hero on EASY and NORMAL below the health floor: by design, but a player who sits at 8 hp next to a fuel truck survives it, decide with the owner; (6) the 125 weapon models are generated placeholders (wave 114); (7) the first-spawn hitch of an unwarmed look is 100 .. 390 ms on a desktop; (8) `test_mission_audit` is load sensitive (see above), `test_ai_tactics2` (68 s), `test_soak_systems` (75 s) and `test_mission_audit` (72 .. 128 s) are the slowest tests of the suite: keep them off the same CI shard.

## Wave 113 CI fixes (CI run 43596584732 on f6c7ade was red: shard 0 two tests, shard 1 two tests and its 30 minute limit; watcher w113-ci-apk)
- `test_free_roam_world` "the raid runs while they live" (both attempts, shard 0): REAL BUG since wave 111. The events are built 170-340 m from the hero (`RoamEvents.MIN_D` / `MAX_D`) and the ambient despawn rule of `AlienWorld.step` removes every creature without a site beyond 1.7 x its ring (136 / 156 / 178 m on LOW / MED / HIGH; air units x 1.5): the raiders were gone after one physics step and the raid counted as WON a second later (`_alive == 0 and t > 1`, "ALIEN RAID DONE +300" for nothing); the guards of a stash were gone before the hero came and the scavengers of a crash are spawned inside the range only. The old test sat at 161 m, so some members of the group survived by chance (the sandbox) or none did (the runner, twice). Fix: `RoamEvents._adopt` marks every creature of an event with the meta `hold` (like the event vehicles), `AlienWorld.step` skips the despawn rule for it, `_cleanup` removes the mark when the event ends. The test now builds the raid 185 m out, checks that every raider and every guard still stands, and finishes the foes in two rounds (`_kill_all`: a foe with a medic near goes down on the first hit and dies on the second, `RosterFoe.die`); measured: 3 / 6 / 7 raiders built, all standing after 0.5 s, the raid ends on the kills.
- Loadout text overflow with a wide font (`test_weapon_ui_v3` x3 checks, `test_ui_kit_screens` x2, `test_menus_map_quality` x1, all at 1280x720): the UI font is a `SystemFont` (Roboto Condensed ... Sans-Serif), which is Inter in this sandbox (fontconfig answers Inter for every name) and DejaVu Sans on the GitHub runner, about 25 % wider. Menu text never shrinks below `BODY_MIN` and `Hud._text` grows the box around a too wide line, so the FX line of a part card ('Big zoom, wide hip') ran into the next card and the class caption ('SHOTGUN  12') into the two chips. Fix: `UiUtil.fit_text` (cut after the last whole word that fits, then "...") used by `MenuKit.part` for the name and the FX line, `LoadoutScreen.class_caption` (name and count, else the name, else cut). REPRODUCE a runner font in this sandbox: a fonts.conf with only `<dir>/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu</dir>` and `<dir>/usr/share/fonts/truetype/liberation</dir>` and a `<cachedir>`, then `FONTCONFIG_FILE=<that file> tools/test.sh tests/test_weapon_ui_v3.gd` (it failed with the same three checks before the fix, passes after); `FONTCONFIG_FILE=... MENU_UNLOCK=1 MENU_ONLY=roster_loadout_rifle BOT_RES=1280x720 tools/bot.sh res://tests/bot_menu_shots.gd ...` renders the screen with it. 18 UI and loadout tests pass with that font; with Inter `test_weapon_ui_v3`, `test_ui_kit_screens`, `test_menus_map_quality`, `test_localisation_complete`, `test_weapons_v3`, `test_alien_world` and `test_free_roam_world` pass.
- Shard 1 ran 1743 s and was cancelled by the 30 minute job limit three seconds after its last test (the other shards 20 to 24 minutes); the limit of the four Godot shards is 45 minutes now. The retries of the four red tests cost about 2.5 minutes of it.
- FLAKY in that run (pass on retry, nothing changed): `test_interior_gameplay` "repeated enter / exit does not leak nodes (4101 -> 4118)" (limit 12; +6, +2, -6 in three local runs), `test_real_fight` "the third wave closes the set: the breather follows", `test_director_round5_b` "zoom 2: no two icons overlap (garage / garage)".
- Round 2 (CI 43601580915 on 2621774): the four Godot shards green (67 / 66 / 66 / 66 tests; 1847 / 1362 / 1684 / 1053 s: the runner was 30 % slower than in the first run and shard 0 needed 31 minutes, which the old 30 minute limit would have cancelled; FLAKY once `test_outskirts_settlements` "a second visit within the cool-down pays nothing (75)"). The visual job failed on one spot, `boss`: "flat white rectangle frac=0.031 rect=(0, 256, 320, 104)" (limit 0.01), a frame that passes in the sandbox and passed in the first CI run, so a white car or van passing the lens (the HANDOFF list of wave 113 already names the flat cream and yellow vans). `tests/bot_visual_qa.gd` now looks again up to `FLAT_LOOKS` = 3 times (24 frames apart, `VQA <spot> RELOOK n`) before a flat rectangle fails any spot but the hero (which has its own re-look); a quad stuck in the frame is still there at the fourth look and fails as before (probed with a forced verdict: three re-looks then FAIL, the counter resets per spot).
- Open: with the DejaVu font the tab labels (MAGAZINE), the tile names (ARC CASTER, ION BATTLE, FLINT RIFLE) and WEAPON INFO still overflow their own boxes by a few pixels at 1280x720 (no neighbour is hit, no check flags it): `UiUtil.fit_text` is the tool; a phone has Roboto Condensed, which is narrower than Inter.

## Wave 114: weapon art and presentation (builder w114-weapons-art; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Weapon kit"; progress log docs/sprint/progress/w114-weapons-art.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/weapon_kit_{lineup_0..5,before_after,in_hand,enemies,enemies_game}.jpg)
- All 229 weapons have their own model made by `godot/tools/blender/weapon_kit.py` (LOD0 hand / menus, LOD1 enemies), one PBR material of 32 swatches (texture arrays), vertex AO, edge wear, glow trims, moving parts (slide, bolt handle, pump, hammer, cylinder, rotary barrels, magazine, coil) driven by `WeaponHandling`. `WeaponModels.build / path / mesh(id, lod) / material_for` are the entry points; the old procedural and open-source meshes stay as `stub_mesh` fallbacks (and QT_WEAPON_KIT=0).
- Tests: NEW `tests/test_weapon_models_v3.gd` (3850 checks, 30 s: every id inside its triangle budget with LOD1, no black or untextured surface, valid layers, the PBR material with its three arrays, markers, distinct silhouettes, plus the real scene: the hero holds a kit weapon with his own material copy and scaled grips, a shot kicks the slide, an empty pistol keeps it back, the magazine leaves on a reload, pump / rotary / revolver motion, the enemy carries the LOD1 model, the shell leaves the eject port). Honest updates: `test_weapons_available_display` and `test_combat_feel` (a hand mine is 0.06 m long, the lance is longer than the scatter gun, grips are those of the drawn weapon, the carbine carries its own grips), `test_open_models` (checks `stub_mesh`, the fallback). Green: test_weapon_models_v3, test_weapons_available_display, test_combat_feel, test_open_models, test_weapons_v3, test_weapon_cards, test_hero_polish, test_enemy_look_v3, test_weapon_roster.
- Perf gate on this tree (tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2, run twice: the first run, in the middle of the wave, was OVER by one and two draws on the 4-enemy death spike of MED / HIGH (151 > 150, 172 > 170) and an A/B with `QT_WEAPON_KIT=0` on MED gave the same 151, so it was not the kit but other builders' changes of the shared tree; the SECOND run at the end is all OK): LOW OK (draws 72, total 88, 134k tris, tex 110 MB, death spike 101), MED OK (96 / 122, 270k tris, tex 141 MB, death spike 143), HIGH OK (112 / 142, 376k tris, tex 206 MB, death spike 164). The kit itself adds +0 draws, about +620 triangles and +1 MB of textures in the street scene (one material, one draw per weapon).
- Look-dev bots (real game, real camera, phone resolution 1600x720): `tests/bot_weapon_art.gd` (the hero with one weapon: idle, aim, burst, empty, reload; `BOT_WEAPON=id`) and `tests/bot_weapon_foes.gd` (one armed roster enemy per picture on the EASTFIELD runway; `WF_IDS=merc_rifle,... WF_FRONT=6.5 WF_SIDE=0 WF_AGGRO=0|1 WF_HUD=0`); both run through `tools/bot.sh` with `ENGINE_ARGS="--fixed-fps 12"` and `QUALITY=0|1`. MED frames cost about 1.5 minutes each on the software renderer.
- Size: glb 18.9 MB raw (5.5 MB gzip), PNG 2.1 MB, repo weight about 21.4 MB raw / 7.6 MB compressed; the APK carries about 6.7 MB of imported scenes and texture arrays (before deflate). New asset folder `godot/assets/models/weapons/`.
- Open for other waves: (1) the hero camera sees a weapon mostly end-on (the stock and the rear of the receiver) from behind the shoulder: a camera or hero scope item; (2) weapon pictures of the menus come from the new meshes through `WeaponModels.mesh` (the weapons-ui builder: use `material_for`); (3) scope reticle and lens look, falling magazine, heat shimmer and class-specific muzzle flash shapes are not done; (4) ceramic energy shells and the pale quantum guns are the weakest models, bows are thin in side view; (5) `weapon_specs.py` is the place to give a weapon a different look (change a row, run the kit with `--ids`, `tools/import.sh`).


## Wave 114: weapon pictures, the Arsenal screen and weapon sound (builder w114-weapons-ui; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Weapon pictures, the Arsenal screen and weapon sound"; progress log docs/sprint/progress/w114-weapons-ui.md; loops docs/shots/round6/weapons_ui_iterations.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/weapons_ui_*.jpg)
- PICTURES: every one of the 229 weapons has a thumb (256 x 128) and a card (512 x 256) picture rendered from its own model (`godot/tools/icons/weapon_pics.sh`; 3/4 view, rim light in the class colour), packed into per-class WebP atlas pages (`godot/assets/icons/wp/`, 37 pages, ETC2 26.7 MB raw / about 7.5 MB deflated in the APK, 6 MB in the repo). `WeaponPics.thumb(id) / card(id)` are `AtlasTexture`s (do not cache them in statics: the thumb pages stay loaded, about 7 MB, the card pages are an LRU of 5). `ItemIcons.texture("w", id)` / `weapon_large` hand them out, so the wheel, the HUD plates, the kill feed (`HudFeed` lines carry `icon`), pickup lines, the crate billboards, the reveal and the lists use them. After ANY model change run `godot/tools/icons/weapon_pics.sh` (stale ids only) and `tools/import.sh`: `tests/test_weapon_pics.gd` fails on a picture older than its model.
- SCREENS: `Arsenal` (scripts/ui/arsenal.gd, arsenal_card.gd, weapon_rings.gd) replaced the paged LOADOUT and the paged catalogue: a virtualised scrolling grid (9 tiles exist of 229), filters ALL / OWNED / NEW / LOCKED / FAVS, class chips, sort, the star, four LOADOUT SLOTS (`LoadoutSlots`; SWAP / `PlayerWeapon.cycle` walk the slots), a card with the picture plate, SEVEN stat bars with a compare marker against the weapon in hand, the range and blast rings to scale beside a person, tabs STATS / PARTS (live stat preview, FIT / TAKE OFF / BUY, the 3D weapon) / AMMO, EQUIP / BUY. A tile tap SELECTS (quick mode, opened by the wheel gesture: one tap takes the weapon and resumes). `CrateReveal` plays about 3.4 s over the HUD when a crate is opened, a weapon is bought (also at the interior arms dealer: it goes through `Progress.buy_weapon`) or a mission pays one; a weapon picked up in a fight stays a HUD line.
- SOUND: 83 sound sets (`WeaponAudio`, `godot/tools/audio/make_weapons_v4.py`, 148 new OGG, 1.3 MB): mechanism click + body + tail on a `Tail` bus whose reverb opens indoors, distance-filtered 3D voices, reload and equip per class, explosions by blast size (small / medium / large / huge), impacts by material (wood, dirt, foliage, water, armor, shield; the world tells them apart in `ImpactSparks.surface_at`, trees are wood with leaves above), ricochets, hit / kill markers, haptics. At least four distinct sets in use per major class. Nothing clips (every file -3 dBFS or lower, the loudest layer stack -4.5 dBFS before the Master limiter).
- TESTS: NEW `tests/test_weapon_pics.gd` (29 checks, 10 s), `tests/test_weapon_audio_v3.gd` (104 checks, 10 s), `tests/test_weapon_ui_v3.gd` REWRITTEN for the Arsenal (about 270 checks, real scene, about 90 s: every weapon has pictures, 229-entry list scrolled by drag / flick / wheel inside a frame budget, filters, sort, favourites, stats against the table for all 229, compare, rings, attachments change the numbers live, ammo, slots and the quick swap, BUY and the reveal hooks, kill feed and crate pictures, the touch wheel, layout scans at 844x390, 1200x540 and 1280x720). Honest updates of old tests to the new flows (scroll list instead of pages, a tile tap selects, FIT / BUY button, the 3D turntable on the PARTS tab or `ars_view3d`, SWAP walks the four slots, window 1280x720 for layout checks): test_weapon_cards, test_weapons_depth, test_menu_cut, test_director_round2_d, test_director_round4_b, test_ui_kit_screens, test_rewards_progression, test_weapons, test_melee_gadgets, test_audio_budget2, test_audio_real_samples, test_audio_redesign (audio folder budget 27 MB), plus es / hi strings (`scripts/common/strings_weapons_v4.gd`).
- Real bugs the render loops found: the reveal overlay had size 0 (nothing drawn), the weapon pop plate hid its own picture, the wheel and the kill feed drew white boxes (atlas page thrash), the hero tabs were registered twice. See the iteration log.
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, run once at the end of the wave on the shared tree, all three OK): LOW draws 70, total 92 (of 100), death spike 107 (of 115), tex 112 MB; MED 97 / 129 (of 130), death spike 149 (of 150), tex 143 MB; HIGH 113 / 149, death spike 170 (of 170), tex 209 MB. The MED and HIGH death spikes sit on their limits: nothing may be added to a frame with four dying enemies without taking something out.
- Size: repo +6 MB of icons, +1.3 MB of audio, about 3 MB of docs (sheets); the APK carries about +7.5 MB (deflated icon pages) +1.3 MB (OGG). Texture memory: the thumb pages 7 MB once the wheel or a list has drawn them, at most 5 card pages (about 5 MB) while a card is on screen, none in the street unless the HUD draws a picture.
- Open: (1) nothing was measured on a phone: the frame costs are the sandbox's software renderer (a redraw of the Arsenal 4 - 8 ms, worst 16 ms over 229 weapons) and the first look at a class loads its atlas page; (2) the kill feed's third line can touch the objective text at the top for long weapon names (old layout); (3) the interior arms dealer reveal was not looked at in a frame; (4) the pictures follow the art builder's models (blocky in places); (5) the `test_director_fixes_b` check "the weapon in hand is drawn thicker ((1.3, 1.3, 1.1))" fails against the art builder's `KIT_LOOK_SCALE` (not touched here); (6) `test_audio` prints a script error for `boom` (an OGG cast to WAV, it was there before).


## Wave 114 critic, PLAY lens (independent critic and fixer; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Wave 114 critic, PLAY lens"; test `tests/test_play_critic_w114.gd`; log docs/sprint/progress/w114-critic-play.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/play_critic_w114_{feed,reveal}_before_after.jpg)
- FIXED (6, each with a regression test that fails without its fix): (1) 56 weapons of the throw kind and 7 charge weapons never played their wave 114 sound sets (grenades, mines and strikes played the QUANTUM BLINK, rocket launchers the gravity gun, the bow the plasma zap): `WeaponAudio.launch / charge_start / fire_charged`, hooks in `_throw`, `_tick_charge`, `_fire_lance`, `BlastOps._weapon_fire`, `EnergyArms.salvo / fan`; (2) `PlayerWeapon._auto_swap` was never called (a dry weapon with no reserve clicked for ever): wired, loadout slots first; (3) the 10 burst weapons fired at half the rate and half the DPS of their card (the last round of a burst added a second interval): `tick`, burst branch; (4) the kill / pickup feed crossed the objective line and the toast: it hangs under the toast lane (`hud.gd`, `HudFeed.MAX_W`); (5) a crate opened in a fight dimmed the whole screen and covered the middle of the view for 3.4 s: `CrateReveal.in_combat()` gives a small 2 s card at the side; (6) every alt-fire ended on the ENTANGLE blip (a slug, a scope switch, a vent): `alt_fire` no longer plays it, SPIN SWEEP and SHOVE play `WeaponMech._swing_sound()` (they had no other sound when they hit nothing).
- Tests: NEW `tests/test_play_critic_w114.gd` (sections sound, ammo, burst) and `tests/test_play_critic_w114_ui.gd` (hud, touch, reveal), about 40 s each; each check was shown to fail without its fix (mutation runs). Honest updates: `test_audio_redesign` (a half charge of the lance plays its own recording `shot_lance`, not the old `shot`), `test_director_fixes_b` (the weapon thickness check asked for 1.3 in double precision, a Vector3 holds 1.2999999: the open item 5 of the weapons-ui builder). Probes and bots (`tests/dev_w114_*.gd`, `tests/bot_w114_*.gd`) are look-dev tools, not part of the suite.
- Open for other waves: the barrel lags the aim line 6 - 13 degrees during a burst while looking steeply down; 15 hitscan weapons need more than the 6 voices of the `shot` family at full auto; the kill-feed icon uses the weapon in hand when the kill is polled (a blast or car kill can show the wrong weapon); the toast can still sit beside the hero's face over the feed; wave 115 snake bombs and wave 116 ordnance should call `WeaponAudio.launch` for the hero's own launches (the set follows `WeaponAudio.set_of(id)`).

## Wave 114 critic, LOOK lens (independent critic and fixer; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Wave 114 critic, LOOK lens"; tests `tests/test_weapon_view_w114.gd`, `tests/test_scope_view_w114.gd`, `tests/test_critic_look_w114.gd`; bot `tests/bot_critic_hand_w114.gd`; log docs/sprint/progress/w114-critic-look.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/w114_critic_look_{hand_before_after,scope_screens}.jpg)
