The headless dummy renderer prints `Parameter "material" is null (material_get_instance_shader_parameters)` for every weapon test: noise, not a defect (test_scope_view filters it).
- Perf gate on this tree (run once, PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2): LOW OK (72 draws, 94 total, death spike 107 of 115, 112 MB), MED OK (97 / 129, death 148 of 150, 143 MB), HIGH OK (113 / 149, death 169 of 170, 209 MB). The scope draws only while a scoped weapon is aimed, the flash is smaller, the weapons cost the same triangles and draws as before.
- For the integrator: the atlas pages of assets/icons/wp/ were repacked (m_shield), run `tools/import.sh` after taking them; `PlayerBody.carry_spread` and `aim_show` are the knobs of the carried and aimed pose (0 turns the new pose off); `WeaponModels._look_cache` is filled on first use (a test that wants the old scale sets it).

## Wave 114 integration (weapon models and animation, weapon screens, icons and sound, two critics)
- What the wave holds: the weapon KIT (229 weapons with their own Blender-made models, one PBR material, class handling and shells: builder section above), the ARSENAL screen with 229 pictures, 83 sound sets and the crate reveal (weapons-ui section), the LOOK critic (carried and aimed pose, muzzle flash, sniper SCOPE view, part cards) and the PLAY critic (63 weapons that never played their sound sets, `_auto_swap` that nothing called, burst weapons at half their rate, the feed over the objective line, the crate reveal in a fight).
- Full suite (`tools/test_all.sh`, one file per call with 3 jobs through a small resumable wrapper because a tool call is limited to 10 minutes; a clean user dir per test through `XDG_DATA_HOME`; 273 tests, 42 minutes, 7551 test-seconds): first run 272 PASS, 1 FLAKY, 0 FAIL. No stale test failed (the builders had moved theirs). FLAKY by name: `test_play_critic_w113` ("81 table entries: an enemy at 0.5, 1 and 1.5 x the radius loses what the table says", la_rocket lost 204.9 where the table says 147.5, and the car variant with gr_well): passes on the retry and 3 of 3 times solo, so it is load sensitive; the cause of the extra damage was not looked at. After the integrator's fixes below the 39 test files that touch the changed code (rigs, enemies, aliens, test mode, soak, blast, weapons) ran again: all PASS.
- Real bugs found by `tools/full_bot.sh` and fixed (the bot is random enough that identical seeds differ: the first run failed, three of the next four passed):
  - A dying MENDER sank to y = -INF (`CharacterRig.lowest_world_y` answered INF for a rig with no hull points, `Operative._tick_dead` put the corpse at `-_rest_drop`): `Condition "!v.is_finite()" is true` (`instance_set_transform`, 977 to 1188 times for the 4 s of the corpse, three render nodes a frame; the engine names no node and prints no script backtrace). It first looked like a wave 114 weapon problem (the errors began in the strike weapons, st_orbital); a baseline of the previous commit (`git archive HEAD` copy, same bot) failed the same way on its second run, so it is OLDER than this wave. The new `NONFINITE` lines of `tests/bot_soak.gd` (`_diag_non_finite`: Node3Ds with a NaN / INF transform, camera rig, launch aim, enemies and vehicles at bad positions) named `/root/Main/EnemyManager/Mender2/ImportedCharacter` with origin y = -inf. Fix: the lowest point of a rig without hulls is the model's own height. `tests/test_character_motion.gd` checks a rig without hulls answers a finite value and kills a mender, checking every 0.25 s that its model stays finite (both fail without the fix: `model y -inf`). Why the mender's `enemy_demon` body has no hull points was not looked at; its corpse now lies at its model origin.
  - `AlienCreature._wake` (`for m: AlienCreature in members`): a pack member freed by an air strike (st_air_l) made every wake a script error ("Trying to assign invalid previously freed instance") and stopped the loop: the same typed-variable bug as the earlier `_separate` fix (commit 2b4c37b). Untyped variable, `is_instance_valid` first; `tests/test_alien_world.gd` wakes a pack whose first member was removed by the world and freed (fails without the fix, with the exact script error).
  - "1 ENEMIES DOWN" (`TestMode.kill_all_enemies`, the toast in the QA frames since wave 108) is "1 ENEMY DOWN".
  - Looked at and NOT kept: a guard for a non-finite aim point in `LaunchAim`, `BlastPreview` and `BlastOps._weapon_fire` (I thought the strike preview was the source: three render nodes a frame fit its ring, wall and footprint; the diagnostics proved it was the mender). A forced NaN aim ray makes the same engine error, so those three places would be the ones to guard if a NaN camera ever shows up.
- `tools/full_bot.sh` on the final tree: PASS (verdict PASS, 0 errors, 12.7 game minutes, headless). Runs of this session: wave 114 tree 6 (the first FAIL had the mender signature but no diagnostics yet, 3 PASS with the mender bug still in, a FAIL on the alien wake after the mender fix, PASS with both fixes); previous commit 2 (PASS, mender FAIL). So the full bot did fail, on code that is not from this wave, and it passes with both fixes. Documented in `docs/QA.md` (items 4 and 5 of the bugs the soak found).
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, nothing else running, clean user dir, the tree before the three small script fixes above, none of which touches rendering): LOW OK (74 draws, 96 total of 100, 140.1 k triangles total of 160 k, tex 112 of 200 MB, death spike 107 of 115, HUD canvas draws 22), MED OK (97 / 129 of 140, 272.2 k of 280 k, tex 143 of 260 MB, death 149 of 150), HIGH OK (113 / 149 of 160, 379.9 k of 430 k, tex 209 of 360 MB, death 170 of 170). A solo LOW run before it gave 71 / 93 total, so the staged scene varies by 2 to 3 draws between runs. HIGH has no headroom in the 4-enemy death spike (170 of 170) and MED has one draw (149 of 150): the next builder to add a draw to a death turns it OVER. The weapon kit itself adds no draw (one material, one draw per weapon).
- `tools/visual_qa.sh`: 27 / 27 pass (run in four pieces because of the 10 minute limit of a tool call, the sheet `docs/shots/latest.png` is rebuilt from all 27 frames with the script's own code). `docs/shots/latest.png` and the hero, boss, car and interior frames looked at full size. Defects: (1) none of the 27 spots shows this wave's new screens (Arsenal, wheel, crate reveal, scope): add spots (with the outskirts and enemy-lineup spots earlier waves asked for); (2) the hero frame (front view) holds the carbine upright against the chest with a glove floating beside it: the hands still do not grip (LOOK critic item 3, a per-grip finger rig is needed); (3) the boss frame still stacks the VAEL OVERSEER bar, CONTRACT FAILED ABANDONED and the HIJACK BUS prompt, and QUANTUM CORES 0 / 6 NEAREST xxx M is on every free frame; (4) the four street frames are wide empty night plazas, a small hero, cream box vans and white sedans that read as toys beside the violet coupe of the car frame (a smooth flat-shaded pink); (5) the four sky frames look at facades, so dawn, noon, dusk and night look alike; (6) in the interior the dealer prompts overlap the hero's pack and a foreground citizen is a brown blob; (7) the title backdrop is very dark. None was a small fix.
- Added weight of the wave (staged commit, bytes): game assets 28.3 MB with their .import files (models 19.3: 229 GLB 18.97 + 232 .import 0.23 + weapons_meta.json 0.08; textures 2.08 (3 weapon atlas PNG); icons 5.57 (36 WebP pages 5.50, 37 .import); audio 1.33 (148 OGG 1.27); shaders 0.01), scripts 0.17 MB, tests 0.21 MB, tools 0.34 MB, contact sheets 5.3 MB in docs/shots/round6 (not exported), no raw archive, no third-party asset. `tools/size_report.sh`: assets total 335 MB (305 at wave 113); no export was made here, the builders' estimate for an APK is +15 MB (imported weapon scenes and texture arrays 6.6, icon pages 7.5 deflated, OGG 1.3). Texture memory 112 / 143 / 209 MB, limits untouched. CI: the 273 files deal out to 69 / 68 / 68 / 68 per shard; my per-test times scaled by the ratio of the wave 113 CI run to the wave 113 sandbox run give about 21 to 24 minutes a shard (limit 45); the nine new or rewritten wave 114 tests add about 4 minutes in all.
- Tidiness: `tests/dev_arsenal_probe.gd` and `tests/dev_dump_snd.gd` (named nowhere) moved out of the repo; the other `dev_w114_*` and `bot_w114_*` probes are named in the builders' logs and stay; no `zz_*` or `*_tmp` file in the tree (my probes were deleted); the generated digests `docs/sprint/progress/auto_*.md` and `AUTO_INDEX.md` are not committed (as in waves 112 and 113). Scratch: 306 test-log temp dirs in /tmp, my baseline copy of the previous commit (2 GB) and my logs deleted; disk 12 GB free.
- Open follow-ups: (1) QA spots for the Arsenal, the wheel, the reveal, the scope, an enemy lineup and an outskirts town (item 1 above); (2) the hands do not grip (carbine finger curl baked into the rig), katana slash zigzag, white railgun wedge, brown stock block at the shoulder (LOOK critic); (3) the barrel lags the aim line 6 to 13 degrees in bursts looking steeply down, 15 hitscan guns exceed the 6 `shot` voices at full auto, the kill-feed icon uses the weapon in hand when the kill is polled, the kill feed's third line can touch the objective text (critics and weapons-ui builder); (4) weapon models: ceramic and quantum energy shells are pale, bows are thin from the side, the hero camera sees a weapon end-on from behind the shoulder, enemy rifles are about 15 px at 7 m (art builder); (5) the mender's `enemy_demon` body has no hull points (why?) and the first wake of a pack after an air strike was never covered by a test before; (6) `test_play_critic_w113` is load sensitive (above), and `tests/test_vehicle_roster.gd` still stops at its line 94 (wave 112 note, not touched); (7) nothing was measured on a phone.

## Wave 114 CI fixes (CI run 43652163904 on b496e61: Three.js, Unreal, visual QA 27 / 27 and shards 2 and 3 green; shard 1 and shard 0 red; watcher w114-ci)
- Shard 1, `test_weapon_ui_v3` (both attempts, ONE check of 271): "loadout, blade stats @1280x720 layout clean ... off screen 'HITS EVERYONE IN THE ARC' [P: (1031, 424), S: (258, 15)]". The melee caption of the Arsenal card (`WeaponRings._melee`) is drawn at the `BODY_MIN` floor that `Hud._text` keeps, so in the runner's DejaVu Sans (about 25 % wider than the sandbox's Inter) the box grew 9 px past the right edge of the screen (the same family as the wave 113 loadout overflow). REPRODUCED here with the DejaVu-only fonts.conf of the wave 113 note: `FONTCONFIG_FILE=<fonts.conf> tools/test.sh tests/test_weapon_ui_v3.gd` failed with exactly that check before and passes after (and with Inter). Fix: the caption keeps its wording when it fits and reads "HITS ALL IN THE ARC" when the font is too wide, `UiUtil.fit_text` as the last guard; nothing changes with Inter. `tests/bot_arsenal.gd` has a new step `stats_blade` for that frame (`ARS_STEPS=stats_blade`).
- Shard 0 (ONE test, failed on both attempts; 69 tests, 1766 s) could not be named in the first run: the job log of a shard is 212 437 lines (`test_ui_debt3` alone prints 196 541 `ok:` lines) and the log API returns only the last 5000 of them (tests 34 to 69, all PASS); the raw log download hosts are not reachable from the sandbox. Tests 1 to 32 of the shard, run here in the shard's own order with a fresh `XDG_DATA_HOME`, the DejaVu font and also in a clean `git archive` copy (no ignored files, fresh import), all PASSED. So `tools/ci_shard.sh` was changed first to name itself: it keeps the output of a failed attempt and, after the last test, prints "what failed" (the FAIL / SCRIPT ERROR / crash lines of the failed attempt, also for FLAKY tests), writes it to the job summary and raises one annotation per failed (`::error`) or flaky (`::warning`) test (a title must not hold a comma or a colon, the runner cuts it there). The annotations are readable without a log download: `GET /repos/<owner>/<repo>/check-runs/<job id>/annotations` (no token for a public repository). Probed with four stub tests (pass, flaky, fail, crash).
- Shard 0, named by those annotations (CI 43658858042 on 878ebf0): `test_outskirts_settlements` "a second visit within the cool-down pays nothing (75)" (both attempts) and `test_vehicles_city` "parked cars stand on the parking bays (8.3 m from the road centre)" (once, FLAKY, passed on the retry: an old traffic-physics load flake, not touched). The first is a TEST RACE, not a game bug. A first visit to a town is paid by TWO ticks: the notice board (`SettlementWorld._use_town`, 75 % of the reward, every 0.15 s of PHYSICS time) and the town's discovery bonus (`OutskirtsWorld._arrive`, the other 25 %: 75 of HARROW FIELD's 300, every 0.25 s of FRAME time). With `Engine.time_scale = 4` and 240 physics ticks the two clocks run at different speeds as the frame rate changes. The test waited for the board only and took its credit baseline then; when the town had not been found yet, the discovery bonus landed about five frames later, inside the 44 frames of the cool-down check, and was counted as a second payment. Here the town is always found earlier: the steps before the notice board run at 1 to 5 frames a second while the world is still building (`Engine.get_frames_per_second()` 1.0, 3.0, 5.0 after the streaming, parked cars and doors steps; at x4 one frame is up to a second of game time), so the discovery tick fires in every frame and HARROW FIELD is found on the way (`found=true` and +75 per town reached before the board): the test passed in every run here. On the runner the town was evidently not found when the board paid, most likely because its frames are shorter (inferred, not measured: a six-frame door visit then lasts a tenth of a game second, less than one discovery tick), so the board's own visit was the first time the hero stood in the town's range for a tick. REPRODUCED in a clean copy by pushing the discovery tick out (`OutskirtsSites.reset_found()` and `city.sites._tick = 0.4` before the first visit): FAIL "(75)" with `found=false` after the board paid and `found=true` at the check; with the fix the same setup PASSES (`dcred` 300 after the wait, 0 at the check). Fix (test only, meaning kept): the wait is for `tw.used` AND `OutskirtsSites.is_found` before the baseline is taken.
- Round 3 (CI 43662334686 on 4d3cce1): Three.js, Unreal, visual QA and shards 1, 2, 3 green; shard 0 red on `test_mission_audit_kinds`, both attempts (attempt 1: "side mission turf / hostage completes by itself (TIME UP, 150 s)"; attempt 2: "no sandbox step gets stuck (1 findings)", a heist UNREACHABLE); FLAKY once and not touched: `test_combat_feel` "a kill plays its confirm sound (hit_armor)" (shard 2, passed on the retry). The test is a long-known flake (waves 79 to 113); it failed 6 of 33 unforced local runs here, and the two causes below were found by running it that often with the weapon, the enemies and the position logged:
  - TURF / HOSTAGE TIME UP (REAL, fixed): the bot walks past a weapon crate and `WeaponCrates.open` equips the crate's weapon. The log of a failing run (`AUDIT_VERBOSE=1`, with the weapon, ammo and enemies added to the SIDEDBG lines) shows `weapon=d_rook ammo=0 reserve=0` for 120 s of the kill stage while every guard keeps its full health (a DMR with 96 spare rounds; `napalm` has 12); the carbine of the passing runs has unlimited rounds. A weapon with a limited reserve also makes the convoy step fail again and again (CONVOY GOT AWAY x3) and the rift hold fail (RIFT LOST x15 with `napalm`). REPRODUCED on demand in a clean copy by equipping the weapon at the start (`FORCE_WEAPON`): `d_rook` gives exactly the two TIME UP checks of the runner, `napalm` the rift and convoy failures. Fix (the bot only, the checks keep their meaning): `Audit.keep_carbine()` in the loops of `play_demo` and `audit_side` (`tests/mission_audit.gd`). With it the forced runs pass (4 of 4) and 12 of 12 unforced runs pass; the test also passes twice in the shared tree.
  - HEIST UNREACHABLE (NOT fixed, about 6 % of the runs here, once of four attempts on the runner): "the walk is blocked N m from the goal at (x, -0.6877, z) ahead: [LOW y=-0.69 ... floor=true hp_vel=(0, 0, 0)]" a few seconds after the heist step begins. The hero stands still for 7 s with nothing hit by the forward rays and recovers when the bot hops him 8 m on. y -0.6877 is also where the hero walks normally in the west of the city (the rift hold ground at x -80 is at -0.68), so it is not "below the street". An `ENVDUMP` probe (rays around the stuck hero) was written but no run caught one. Open for whoever owns the heist start: look at where `CampaignRunner._teleport` puts the hero after a convoy step that timed out (the convoy cars of the aborted step may still stand at the market).

## Wave 115 snake combat (builder w115-snake-combat: the verbs, the bombs, the wheels, the reactions; the architecture section is the last one of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`)
- What it is: 10 snake VERBS (pull, grab and throw, disarm, yank cover, net, lash, shield, decoy, trap, guard) and 12 snake BOMBS (coil, swarm, flare, freezing viper, seeker, chain, cluster, constrictor mine, burrower, gravity, storm cobra, mega python), every bomb a `BlastTable` entry of the category `snake`; they sit in the weapon wheel as the SNAKE and BOMBS wedges and as four quick casts on the ABILITY wheel; ammo-free, limited by the snake energy bar of the traversal wave; skill column SNAKE COMBAT; enemy answers (panic, a rifleman shoots the tendril, jammers, the SNAKE HUNTER, civilians film); two Mission Board missions (SNAKE ARENA, ROOFTOP SNAKE HUNT); three first-minutes hints. Code `scripts/quantum/snake_{rows,swarm,aim,holds,verbs,bombs,react,war,icons,ui,war_missions}.gd`.
- Tests: `tests/test_snake_combat.gd` (130 checks, real scene, about 70 s, retries a bomb on other lanes of the random city). Existing tests moved for the additions (the drawn disc of a crowded wheel, the two wedges, the four ability casts, the snake bombs going off through the table): `test_menu_cut`, `test_hud_camera_final`, `test_new_verbs`, `test_weapon_ui_v3`, `test_blast_arsenal`, `test_skill_tree`, `test_hud_declutter`, `test_energy_destructive_scene`; localisation `scripts/common/strings_snake_war.gd`. 27 related tests were run twice and pass; `test_snake_traversal`, `test_new_verbs` and `test_weapon_ui_v3` were red or flaky only while another agent rendered on the same machine.
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, once, at the end): tier 0 LOW 70 draws, 128k triangles, 112 MB, verdict OK; tier 1 MED 97 draws, 241k, 143 MB, OK; tier 2 HIGH 113 draws, 327k, 209 MB, OK. The free snakes are ONE MultiMesh draw (LOW 16 bodies of 216 triangles at most); with a power running LOW adds 2 to 5 draws, the mega python 19.
- Added weight: 24 OGG (250 KB, `assets/audio/snw_*.ogg`) and one icon (`assets/icons/e_hunter.png`, a few KB); no texture, no model; about 6 MB of review sheets under `docs/shots/round6/snake_combat_*.jpg` (not in the APK).
- Look loops: 12 rounds at LOW and MED with the phone HUD (list and defects in `docs/shots/round6/snake_combat_iterations.md`).
- Open: (1) `fx_threat.gdshader`: the stealth pips (mode 4) are caught by the health-bar branch (`if (v_data.w > 2.5)`) and drawn as white plates over every suspicious enemy; the fix is `v_data.w > 2.5 && v_data.w < 3.5` there (not my file: not changed); (2) `snake_pose.gd:182` (traversal) prints "Basis must be normalized" after a zip to a car; (3) no cast animation for the hero (the arms keep the rifle hold, the hands glow); (4) a pooled enemy keeps a freeze or shock overlay of the stage before for its remaining seconds when `EnemyManager.clear()` is followed by a spawn (look-dev bots call `DamageWorld.current.foes.reset()`); (5) nothing measured on a phone.


## Wave 115 critic, PLAY lens (independent critic and fixer; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Wave 115 critic, PLAY lens"; test `tests/test_play_critic_w115.gd` (76 checks, about 60 s); probes `tests/dev_w115p_*.gd`, bots `tests/bot_w115p_*.gd`; log docs/sprint/progress/w115-critic-play.md)
- Fixed: (1) the gun never came back after a snake power was armed from the weapon wheel (swap, number keys and the wheel left the power in the hand: `SnakeWar.holster()` from `PlayerWeapon.poll_switch`, `WeaponWheel.activate` and the `weapon_changed` signal, plus a one-time hint); (2) a tap of SNAKE in the air was cancelled before the snake bit (the shot is committed, every swing hangs 0.55 s at least); (3) SNAKE ARENA / HUNT replaced a world mission of the lodge board (`test_world_missions` failed 1 of 113 checks: fixed); (4) the tutorial's FLY and LAND steps could not be done on HARD and QUANTUM (flight is open while the tutorial runs); (5) the snake hunter spawned inside a building or behind a wall in 18 % of the tries; (6) the first swing, verb and bomb compiled 15 pipelines on the spot (the new `SnakeWarm` is part of `ShaderWarmup.run`: 6 left; the first swing still compiles about 8, mostly the view from 40 m up); (7) `Basis must be normalized` in `snake_pose.gd`; (8) the tutorial's swing legs had no anchor above the roof edge in 7 of 12 cases (the held button turned into a flight): `SnakeMissions.swing_possible` + `_pick_swingable` choose roofs with an anchor that are at most 8 m higher (18 of 33 first legs have one now, 11 before; the old pick stays when none has).
- Measured OK: wall climb 45 of 45 buildings, flight and landing, death and respawn in every state, tier switches mid swing, 64 verb x enemy trials against live enemies, twelve bomb rings, 34 monkey episodes (random thumbs, armed powers, live enemies, traffic).
- Open: about 45 % of the boards have no level roof with an anchor within reach of the first roof (the old pick stays: the hold turns into a flight); the first swing still compiles about 8 pipelines (the view from 40 m up); the mega bomb ring promises more than its damage; `fx_threat.gdshader` stealth pips drawn as white plates (not wave 115 code). The LOOK critic fixed the camera lens jumps and the glow that did not react to swing, zip, climb and flight (both were on my first open list). Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, once): LOW 71 draws / 131k / 112 MB OK, MED 97 / 241k / 143 MB OK, HIGH 113 / 328k / 209 MB OK. For the integrator: `tools/import.sh` first (new class `SnakeWarm`), then `test_play_critic_w115`, `test_world_missions`, `test_snake_combat`, `test_snake_traversal`, `test_snake_swing_run`, `test_perf_budget` and `test_hero_polish` (they call `ShaderWarmup.run`).


## Wave 115 critic, LOOK lens (independent critic and fixer; details in godot/ARCHITECTURE.md "Wave 115 critic, LOOK lens"; test `tests/test_critic_look_w115.gd` (about 60 s, every fix has an old-behaviour trial); look-dev bot `tests/bot_critic_snake.gd`, headless probe `tests/dev_critic_snake_play.gd`; sheets docs/shots/round6/critic115_look_*.jpg; log docs/sprint/progress/w115-critic-look.md)
- Fixed: (1) the hero was flung 6 to 12 m in ONE frame during a swing (rope reel debt while a facade or the street held him: `SnakeSwing.reel_slack`, `max_correction`); (2) the camera arm was cut from 3.7 m to 1.2 m in one frame at 30 m/s (2 to 5 m pops): drawn in over three frames while a snake state runs (`CameraRig.snake_pull_in`; on foot unchanged; this was the PLAY critic's open "lens jumps" item, it is real, not a harness artifact); (3) "ENTER VEHICLE" plate over a swinging or flying hero (`Hud.hint_text` fallback measured the flat distance); (4) the wing tips went through facades (`SnakeFlight._keep_out_of_walls`); (5) boots, belt, pack and cloth were flat black from behind (rim in `hero_body.gdshader`); (6) the suit glow never reacted to swing, zip, climb and flight (`SnakeTraversal._glow_tick` drives the `HeroLook` layers `flow` and `wings`: this was also on the PLAY critic's open list); (7) the SNAKE button reads LET GO while swinging; (8) in a barrel roll the wings stayed level while the hero spun (`SnakeFlight._wing_tip` takes the roll basis now; a roll needs a nearly pure sideways double tap, with forward held the stick gives 0.707 < 0.8: PLAY lens).
- Measured: 90 s headless runs (same route): hero jumps 1 -> 0 (11.3 m), camera pops 12 -> 3 (max 2.2 -> 1.2 m), vehicle plate frames 223-760 -> 0; swing run test unchanged (7 and 8 swings, 520 m); perf gate LOW 70 / 128k / 112 MB, MED 97 / 241k / 143 MB (death 149 of 150), HIGH 113 / 328k / 209 MB (death 169 of 170): all OK, nothing added.
- Open: the SNAKE / FLY buttons are crammed between RELOAD, AIM and FIRE (6 px of air; at 844x390 BOOST is clipped by the FIRE ring) and the ABILITY wheel has 11 tiny discs (7 px labels): a layout decision, `test_hud_camera_final` pins it; wings and ropes read as film strips up close; city art seen from the swing (an empty flat plaza at (205, 0, 0), a faceted rooftop orb, flat near-field facades, MED facade cables). For the integrator: `test_critic_look_w115`, `test_hud`, `test_hud_camera_final`, `test_snake_traversal`, `test_snake_swing_run`, `test_play_critic_w115` passed here; the hero shader change is the rim on the dark suit gear (run `test_hero_polish` and visual QA once), the tendril shader slims a body within 3 m of the lens. `test_play_critic_w115` failed one check in one of two runs here ("none stands inside a building": the random hunter spawn of the PLAY critic's own test), it passed on the re-run.

## Wave 115 snake traversal (builder w115-snake-traversal: swing, zip, climb, flight and the movement kit; the architecture section is "QUANTUM SNAKE traversal" of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`; progress log docs/sprint/progress/w115-snake-traversal.md; sheets docs/shots/round6/snake_{tendril,swing,zip_climb,flight,poses,hud}*.jpg, 17 render-look-fix loops at LOW and MED)
- What it is: ONE contextual SNAKE button (action `snake`, Tab) and ONE FLY / LAND button (action `fly`): SNAKE SWING (a pendulum on a rope, AUTO CHAIN), SNAKE ZIP, SNAKE CLIMB (walls, ceilings, a mantle onto the roof), SNAKE FLIGHT (wing snakes, boost, glide, dive, barrel roll, crash), plus the movement kit (double jump, super jump from a standing start, air dash, safe landing roll). Code `scripts/quantum/snake_{tendril,tendril_pool,traversal,swing,zip,climb,flight,anchor,power,pose,camera,fx,missions,mission_director}.gd`, shaders `snake_tendril`, `snake_speed_lines`, `snake_marker`, 12 `snk_*.ogg` (`tools/audio/make_snake.py`), one-line hooks in `player.gd`, `player_body.gd`, `camera_rig.gd`, `hero_verbs.gd`, `game.gd`, `sfx.gd`, `touch_controls.gd`, `mission_board.gd`, `skill_screen.gd`, `specialist.gd`, `ui_settings.gd`, `options_screen.gd`; the HeroPower rows and the skill verb `snake` (the fifth column). The shared tendril for the combat builder: `SnakeTendril.fire(origin, target, opts)`, ONE MultiMesh draw for every snake in the game.
- Enemy counters: snipers lead a swinging or flying hero (`SnakeTraversal.lead_offset`), the warden's pulse jams the snakes 1 / 2 / 3 / 3.5 s by difficulty (HERO MODE is immune). The AIM ASSIST option also drives the snake assist (the options tabs are full). The first perf gate found MED over budget (death spike 151 of 150): the idle effects node cost 2 draws, it is made lazily now; a snake state costs +1 draw.
- Tests: `tests/test_snake_traversal.gd` (135 checks, real scene, about 110 s) and `tests/test_snake_swing_run.gd` (the real city: STORY and NORMAL 6 to 11 swings, 515 to 520 m, no ground). Probes `tests/bot_snake*.gd` and `tests/dev_snake_*.gd` (`dev_snake_routes.gd` measures the chain reliability). The super jump was limited to a standing start because a held JUMP + forward (the vault / mantle habit of a thumb) broke `test_traversal`.
- Measured: the swing run's frames average 16.6 to 17.0 ms headless, p99 24 to 31 ms, worst 34 to 365 ms (a chunk streaming under the hero); `SnakeTraversal.tick` 0.12 ms per physics tick; GPU frame time cannot be measured under software Vulkan. Added weight: 98 641 bytes of OGG, 8 KB of shaders, about 150 KB of source, no texture.
- Open (builder's list): random downtown routes reach 5 swings and 400 m about 55 % of the time (the test route 8 of 8); a zip pull shows the boots at the frame edge for a few frames; only the sniper and the warden react to the snakes; the building-corner neon strips read as a pillar of light up close and the facade cable decor as floating wires at MED (city art); the tutorial LAND step can finish while flying low (reach tolerance 6 m). Its "outside my scope" items: `SnakeWarMissions.inject` overwrote a world mission (fixed by the PLAY critic), the `Parameter "material" is null` noise of the headless dummy renderer (not ours), `tools/import.sh` after a new `class_name`.

## Wave 115 integration (quantum snakes: swing, zip, climb, flight, pull, grab, nets, shield, bombs; traversal and combat builders and two critics)
- What the wave holds: the traversal (builder section above), the snake combat (10 verbs, 12 bombs in the `BlastTable`, the SNAKE and BOMBS wedges and four quick casts on the wheels, reactions, the SNAKE HUNTER, two board missions: section "Wave 115 snake combat"), the PLAY critic (the gun that never came back after a power was armed from the wheel, a tap of SNAKE that never bit, the tutorial's FLY and LAND impossible on HARD, the board mission that replaced a world mission, the hunter inside walls, 15 pipelines compiled by the first swing, `SnakeWarm`) and the LOOK critic (the hero flung 6 to 12 m in one frame by the rope debt, the camera arm cut 2 to 5 m in one frame, the ENTER VEHICLE plate over a flying hero, wing tips through facades, black boots from behind, the suit glow that never answered the snakes).
- Full suite (`tools/test_all.sh` logic through a small resumable wrapper, because a tool call is limited to 10 minutes: one file at a time, 3 jobs, a clean `XDG_DATA_HOME` per attempt, one retry; 278 files, 7 slices, about 50 minutes, 8377 test-seconds of final attempts; the wave adds 5 files to the 273 of wave 114): first run 273 PASS, 3 FLAKY, 2 FAIL. The new tests pass under that load: `test_snake_traversal` 112 s, `test_snake_swing_run` 70 s, `test_snake_combat` 60 s, `test_play_critic_w115` and `test_critic_look_w115` 41 s each; `test_world_missions`, `test_perf_budget` and `test_hero_polish` (the ones that call `ShaderWarmup.run`) pass.
  - FLAKY by name (passed on the retry, none of them touches this wave's code): `test_combat_feel` "a kill plays its confirm sound (hit_armor)" (the long-known one since wave 79); `test_interior_tasks_ui` "copy: the stealth job is on () followers 0 queued 2 aware guards 0 guards 2 mission active false can_start false" (the stealth offer waits for a quiet room: a state race); `test_superhero_hero` "the female body stands on the floor too (-0.119)" (a pose timing check: 3 copies in parallel, 9 runs: this tree 4 pass, a copy of HEAD 3 pass, the takedown lunge, the flinch and the foot level all depend on the frame time; HEAD is as flaky).
  - FAIL on both attempts, both load sensitive, both passing alone (35 s and 55 s) and now fixed or explained, no check weakened:
    - `test_interior_gameplay` "repeated enter / exit does not leak nodes (4037 -> 4056)" and "(4070 -> 4085)" (alone 4029 -> 4032). A probe copy that printed the node paths before and after the six enter / exit cycles (three copies in parallel) showed the extra nodes are `World/Terrain/FarTiles/Far_x_y`: the terrain's far tiles arrive one a frame once its map picture is drawn (the streamer `test_ambient` waits for since the wave 112 CI fix) and fell into the counting window on a loaded machine. Not a leak, not wave 115. Fix (test only, the check is unchanged): `_settle_terrain()` waits for the map, every far tile and no job before the baseline is taken, and the test declares `TIMEOUT_SECONDS` 120. Verified 3 of 3 in parallel with a mission board run beside them (the wait took 7 to 9 s, 4031 -> 4031, 4153 -> 4153, 4121 -> 4121).
    - `test_mission_board`: TWO failure modes. (1) "the story chapter runs through the campaign runner" (it also failed once on the HEAD copy): after the tap on STORY the test waited two frames, but the result card of the sixth mission was still queued with its 1.4 s delay and opened as soon as the board closed, which pauses the game (the message now names the states: "board story, runner active, game paused"); at time scale 16 on a loaded machine two frames are enough for the card. The test empties the card queue before it starts the story and waits on the state (8 s) instead of two frames: 0 failures of this kind in 24 runs on this tree after the fix, 3 in the 42 runs of the two A/B series before it (2 on this tree, 1 on the HEAD copy). (2) "the bot hit at most two stuck findings (3)" and "the first job was finished (0 done)": the audit bot (`tests/mission_audit.gd`) gets stuck on a step now and then under load. NOT fixed, and NOT shown to be this wave's: three copies in parallel, clean user dir, 45 runs on this tree (40 pass, 3 stuck failures) against 36 on a copy of HEAD (33 pass, 2 stuck failures); findings per run 0.56 against 0.28 (25 in 45, 10 in 36: twice as many, p about 0.1, so not significant, but worth a look before the next wave that touches movement or the audit bot). The findings of `mission_audit.gd` now carry the hero's state and what touches him (`_hero_state`, `_surround`: velocity, floor, snake and traversal state, cover, wall run, wade, game state, the forward key, where the camera looks against the path, the colliders within 1.3 m). What they showed: (a) the hero stands at y = -0.6877, UNDER the street (the terrain plate of the world; the same y as the heist case of the wave 114 note, also on HEAD), pressing forward at speed 0 with nothing in front (once with `wall_running` set): probably a hop of the bot (`teleport_to`) that landed where the street collider was not built yet; (b) the hero idles with no key pressed on a smash or kill_target step (progress 0.4 to 0.8, or 2 to 4 foes alive) for 150 to 240 s with a clean state (floor, no snake state): the bot's own logic. Both are old.
  - THE NEW TESTS UNDER LOAD, and two REAL GAME BUGS they led to (3 copies of the same test in parallel, first runs: `test_snake_traversal` 2 of 6 red, `test_snake_combat` 2 of 14 (three different checks), `test_play_critic_w115` 1 of 6, `test_critic_look_w115` 1 of 6; all four passed in the suite run, and `test_snake_combat` failed its first attempt under the runner's DejaVu fonts: the fonts were not the cause). Each flake was followed to its cause:
    - THE TUTORIAL'S LAND RING COULD BE UNREACHABLE (`SnakeMissions.build`, `swing_city`): the last ring was put 90 m past the air ring at street level without looking at the city, so about one board in five put it inside a block (the failing run: the nearest street 20.7 m from the ring, the ring's radius 14 m; `teleport_to` pushed the hero out to the road at x = 80): LAND could not be done and the guide's 90 s stuck handler was the only way out, the owner's "tasks do not complete" in a new place. Fix: the ring goes to `City.road_point_near(land, 0, 50)` when the city has a road there (else as before). After it the tutorial checks were clean in 6 of 6 loaded runs (one of them hit the runner's 140 s cap: three copies of the heaviest test at once).
    - A SNAKE HUNTER COULD STAND INSIDE A STALL, A KIOSK OR A LOW BUILDING (`SnakeReact._try_hunter`): `Drone.activate` puts every spawn at y = its hover (0) whatever y was asked for, while the spot finder accepted the top of any collider up to 4 m high and roofs up to 10 m above the hero (a probe of 40 spawns: one hunter inside a 1.6 m object; the roofs put him inside the building under them). It was the PLAY critic's own flaky check "none stands inside a building (1)". Fix: the hunter is put on the surface the ray found (`h.global_position.y = at.y`). 120 spawns after: 0 inside, 4 stand on low roofs now.
    - Test-only changes, meaning kept: `test_snake_combat`: the hunter check called `_try_hunter` while the game's own half-second pacing timer (`_hunter_try_t`) was still running (17 of 17 calls in a probe that called it again at once did nothing), so it passed only when the timer had run out by luck: the test restarts the timer and calls up to four times; the whiff and trap checks (a bench, a pickup or a car under the crosshair, the global gap of the last cast; one miss each in 14 runs, none in 75 probe lanes) get up to three lanes and print the cause of a miss (`WHIFF TRY`, `TRAP TRY` lines); 9 of 9 green under load afterwards. `test_critic_look_w115`: the "old look" wing tip was read once from a per-frame cache that can still hold the clipped tip (0.69 m instead of more than 1.0): the cache is cleared and the farthest of 12 frames counts; 6 of 6 green. `test_snake_traversal`: the real flight to the air ring tries up to three times (the later ones aim 8 m high until the ring is near: a pilot that only aims is stopped by a facade of a random route) and the ring is placed when none arrives, so that the chapter's later checks still run (the first run of 6 failed there twice, once with the hero hovering 36 m from the ring at 81 m height until the 900 frame limit). The final regression slice (16 tests touching the changed files) passed on the first attempt, three jobs in parallel.
  - `test_full_quick` sits on its limit in this sandbox: 139 s in the suite (PASS), 141 s alone through `tools/test.sh` (timed out at the runner's 140 s cap), 150 s on this tree and 155 s on HEAD with `--timeout=400` (both verdict PASS, 0 errors): the machine is slower than the cap, wave 115 did not slow it down. `test_mission_audit` took 110 to 139 s. CI runs one test at a time on a faster machine and passed both at wave 114.
- Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, nothing else running, the tree before this integrator's three small game changes (the skills screen headers, the hunter's height, the LAND ring's place), none of which adds a draw): all three OK.
  - `PERF RESULT tier=0 label=LOW renderer=mobile touch=0 draws=74 prims=138381 total_draws=96 total_prims=139247 nodes=2931 tex_mb=112 lights=3 hud_draws=21 death_draws=107 death_prims=137075 enemies=4 verdict=OK`
  - `PERF RESULT tier=1 label=MED renderer=mobile touch=0 draws=97 prims=240566 total_draws=129 total_prims=272166 nodes=3070 tex_mb=143 lights=9 hud_draws=24 death_draws=148 death_prims=267053 enemies=4 verdict=OK`
  - `PERF RESULT tier=2 label=HIGH renderer=mobile touch=0 draws=113 prims=327914 total_draws=149 total_prims=380136 nodes=3188 tex_mb=209 lights=7 hud_draws=25 death_draws=169 death_prims=374494 enemies=4 verdict=OK`
  - Headroom is as thin as at wave 114: MED death spike 148 of 150, HIGH 169 of 170 (the next builder to add a draw to a death turns a tier OVER). Texture memory 112 / 143 / 209 MB, limits untouched. The builders' own runs gave LOW 70 to 74 draws (the staged scene varies by 2 to 4 draws between runs).
- `tools/visual_qa.sh`: 27 / 27 pass (in three pieces of 7, 7 and 13 spots because of the 10 minute limit of a tool call; the sheet `docs/shots/latest.png` is rebuilt from all 27 frames with the script's own code). `docs/shots/latest.png` and the hero, street, boss, interior and skill frames looked at full size. Defects: (1) FIXED, the SKILLS screen: wave 115 made it six columns (a column is 200 px wide at 1280 x 720) and "QUANTUM TELEPORT" ran into SUPERPOSITION, "QUANTUM SNAKE" into SNAKE COMBAT. Menu text keeps its floor (it never shrinks), so a header that does not fit now sheds its leading words (`SkillScreen._head`: TELEPORT, SNAKE; the name is translated first, so every language sheds its own words); `tests/test_visual_fixes4.gd` measures the six headers at 844x390, 1200x540 and 1280x720 (a header of several words is never wider than its column; a single long word cannot shrink and is left alone, so the runner's wider DejaVu does not fail it on SUPERPOSITION) and first shows the old look through `SkillScreen.shed_headers = false` (it fails there: 250 > 191 and 209 > 191 px). ## Wave 116 touch audit (agent w116-touch-audit: every control pressed by a real finger; log docs/sprint/progress/w116-touch-audit.md with the findings table; architecture section "Wave 116 touch audit" of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`)
- What it is: `godot/tests/touch_audit_lib.gd` (the engine: the real main scene as an 844x390 phone, a finger made of `InputEventScreenTouch` / `InputEventScreenDrag` in window coordinates, a crawler that walks the menu graph by pressing, the hit area of every control in real pixels) and three permanent tests on it: `tests/test_touch_audit.gd` (the play HUD on foot, MORE, car, helicopter, plane, gunship, tank, snake flight, interior, both wheels, the garage, pause, minimap, the BACK key), `tests/test_touch_audit_menus.gd` (title and pause menu crawl: 22 screens, 300 controls) and `tests/test_touch_audit_screens.gd` (the first minute after PLAY, the map with pan and pinch, ALL EXTRAS, photo mode, the TEST and DEMO menus, the vendor window, the fast-travel list, the result card, the death screen). Bot with pictures: `tests/bot_touch_audit.gd`. About 640 controls are pressed; none is dead, no screen traps the player, no plain Button fires twice.
- Fixed (each with a static switch and an old-behaviour trial in the tests): (1) the phone's BACK key closed the game everywhere, the open map included (the engine default `quit_on_go_back`): new `scripts/ui/back_key.gd` steps one screen back, resumes, pauses, and leaves the app only from the plain title; (2) a tap that is down and up inside one rendered frame never reached the game (the action was pressed and released before a physics tick: SNAKE, FLY / LAND, FIRE, JUMP at 13 to 30 FPS): `TouchControls.hold_fast_taps`; (3) menu rows were 34 px targets on an 844x390 phone: `Hud._button_at` answers in at least 44 px around every plate (`UiUtil.TOUCH_TARGET`, `Hud.finger_targets`), the vendor's CLOSE was 27 px; (4) a story card swallowed a tap on PAUSE (three cards after PLAY and after DEMO): `Story.pause_passes`.
- Open: 120 menu targets stay at 36 to 43 px (rows packed tighter than 82 base px: OPTIONS > GRAPHICS and ACCESS, the CONTROLS page, Arsenal chips, GEAR loadout rows, SUIT swatches; pinned by `KNOWN_SMALL` in the menus test, the count may only go down), the ability wheel's 11 discs are 38 px, the garage paints 48 x 35 px: a layout job (wave 137). Not in the audit yet: cutscene SKIP, tutorial cards, the benchmark panel, most of photo mode's controls. For the integrator: `tools/import.sh` first (new class `BackKey`); run tests that share `user://` one user dir each (`XDG_DATA_HOME`), `test_touch_buttons_r7` fails two checks beside another test in the same dir.

## Wave 116 CI fixes (watcher w116-ci-apk)
- CI run 44847781823 on 643a3b2: Three.js, Unreal, visual QA and shards 0 and 2 green; shard 1 and shard 3 red, one test each, both attempts. Both are TEST RACES against the runner's speed and its live traffic, no game bug; the check-run annotations named them and the job log is downloadable now (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/actions/jobs/<job id>/logs`).
- Shard 3, `test_touch_audit_screens` "a finger dragged over the map pans it": `MapScreen._on_map_input` drops every pointer event in the first `INPUT_QUIET` (0.25 s) after the map opens, so that the tap that opened it places no waypoint. The test waited ten FRAMES after the tap on the MAP card; here ten frames last longer than 0.25 s, on the runner they are shorter, so the finger-down was dropped and the drag had no press to belong to. REPRODUCED here by setting `_open_age` to 0 before the drag (exactly that one FAIL of 124 checks). Fix (test only): wait until `_open_age >= MapScreen.INPUT_QUIET` before the drag. PASS.
- Shard 1, `test_shooting_accuracy` "a grenade or launcher round lands where the crosshair is: worst 27.8 / 30.1 m off" (only "gravity 25 m"; the other three throws 0.2 to 0.3 m): the grenade launcher is a lock-on weapon (`LaunchAim._pick_lock`: an enemy or a VEHICLE within 7.5 degrees of the crosshair and in range; `_throw` then throws at `_launch.landing`). `_throw` is called by the test right after `aim_at`, so it reads the lock of an EARLIER frame (the wait of the 12 m throw, the same lane): a car of the live traffic down the lane was locked on the runner, never here (inferred from the code and the numbers, the log does not print the lock). REPRODUCED here with a node of the group `vehicle` planted 52 m down the lane for that throw: FAIL "17.8 m off"; with the fix the same planted node gives 1.5 m and PASS. Fix (test only, the 2.5 m limit unchanged): the check is the UNLOCKED throw, so the test clears `_launch.target` before `_throw()`. The lock itself is covered elsewhere and was not changed.

## Wave 117 swing (agent w117-swing: the motion and the input of the SNAKE SWING; log docs/sprint/progress/w117-swing.md)
- What it is: `godot/scripts/quantum/swing_motion.gd` (class `SwingMotion`, static functions on a `SnakeSwing`). `SnakeSwing.old_motion = true` is the wave 116 motion (kept for the old-behaviour trial). The pendulum swings in the vertical plane of the travel direction: its centre `SnakeSwing.pivot` is the anchor moved sideways into that plane; `SnakeSwing.anchor` stays the point on the building the rope is DRAWN to (so the drawn length is kept and nothing pulls the hero sideways into the facade that holds the rope: measured 11 m in 1.4 s before). The rope only gets shorter (slack take-up, reel 16 m/s when the arc would come nearer the street than 2.2 to 4.5 m). No speed multiplier anywhere: gravity, an assist toward `SwingMotion.bottom_speed()` (NORMAL 29.7 m/s at the lowest point), hard cap 42 m/s.
- Input: finger up = thrown along the velocity (+1.5 m/s up near the front of the arc); JUMP = let go x 1.12 and +5 m/s up (also while the snake still travels; the still-held SNAKE fires nothing after a JUMP); held SNAKE = lets go by itself 0.62 rad past the lowest point and fires the next rope at the top of the throw; a TAP (finger off within 0.25 s) = one whole swing; a new press between two swings WAITS for an anchor up to 3 s (was: a miss, then SNAKE FLIGHT 0.45 s later); a held chain never turns into flight; the stick steers left / right, back brakes; a braked / still rope (under 2.5 m/s for 0.7 s) lowers the hero at 7 m/s and lets go over the ground; a body on a facade for 0.2 s or on the floor lets go; the drawn rope that meets a corner moves its end there (`wraps`).
- Anchor search (`SnakeAnchors`, new motion): ahead only (fan +-0.95 rad, 6 pitch rows), scored for the planar swing (`SwingMotion.score`), arc checked in the plane (`SwingMotion.arc_clear_time`); a chain with nothing ahead turns 45 / 85 degrees only when the street is 9 m below, never back. `_heading()` = the camera's look bent by the stick.
- After a release: the throw keeps its speed with the thumbs off (`air_k_scale`), an air brake under 10 m (to 11 m/s over the ground, 15 m/s down), a landing roll out of every swing landing faster than 7.5 m/s. The tick a swing lets go no longer moves the body twice (0.95 m in one tick at 28 m/s).
- Check: `godot/tests/test_swing_w117.gd` (real fingers, the real main scene, a recorder per physics tick; 38 checks PASS; `SWING_OLD=1`: 13 FAIL). Probe `tests/dev_w117_anchors.gd`; strip bot `tests/bot_w117_swing.gd` (frames `docs/shots/round7/swing/swing_w117_strip_low.jpg`).
- TEST NOTE: `Engine.time_scale = N` alone makes the physics dt N/60 (the wave 116 probe at 3x measured a 1/20 s game); set `Engine.physics_ticks_per_second = 60 * N` too, and wait a rendered frame after `Input.parse_input_event` (events arrive per frame, not per tick).
- Not done here (the rope agent w117-rope owns snake_zip.gd and the cues): defects 1 to 5 of the wave 116 list (ZIP ends in CLIMB, zip at 80 to 100 m/s, no-anchor cue). Defects 11 to 15 (FLY absent on the ground, LAND toggle, energy warning, no DOWN button, BOOST beside RELOAD) are layout / flight, not the swing: open.

## Wave 117 rope (agent w117-rope: the rope of snakes, the zip that never hangs, the cues; log docs/sprint/progress/w117-rope.md; architecture section "Wave 117 rope" of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`)
- What it is: (1) the swing and zip rope is THREE SNAKES BRAIDED TOGETHER (cyan, green, violet), every one with its own head biting the anchor; the braid flies out loose, pulls tight under load and unwinds as it is let go. Same tendril pool, same ONE draw: each strand is one more instance of the MultiMesh (`SnakeTendril` option `braid`, `snake_tendril_pool.gd`, `snake_tendril.gdshader`). (2) A ZIP at a wall no longer ends hanging in CLIMB: the snakes bite the roof edge and haul the hero up the facade and over it, he ends standing (`SnakeZip` phase `haul`, up to 140 m in at most 3.2 s); with no ledge above he is dropped cleanly and told why. (3) Cues on the touch HUD, no draw call more (`SnakeFx.hud_cues`): a ring on the anchor a ZIP would take with what will happen ("ZIP ROOF +28 M", "WALL NO LEDGE"), NO ANCHOR on the button and at the crosshair after a press that found nothing, ENERGY LOW / WINGS FAIL IN 3 with a ring on LAND before the flight energy ends. (4) Rain in the air: the lens rain of MED / HIGH fades out and the streaks thin while the hero is on his snakes or in the air (`Atmosphere.air_lens`, `RainField.AIR_SHARE`).
- Found in my own frames and fixed: the hero rode up the haul HEAD DOWN (the pull pose aims at a point that lies below him in a haul: `SnakePose` haul pose); a `pow(negative, 2.0)` in the shader glued the three heads into one; the first cue lines were white on a white sky (plates).
- Checks: `godot/tests/test_rope_w117.gd` (25 checks, real fingers, 1200x540; every part once more with its old-behaviour switch off, where the same checks fail: `SnakeTendril.braided_rope`, `SnakeZip.wall_end`, `SnakeZip.haul_max`, `SnakePose.haul_upright`, `SnakeFx.cues_on`, `Atmosphere.air_clear`). Pictures: `godot/tests/bot_w117_rope.gd`, frames `docs/shots/round7/rope_w117/{low,med}`, sheet `docs/shots/round7/rope_w117.jpg`.
- Hook lines in files of other owners (keep them): `snake_swing.gd` (`"braid": 3` in the rope's fire line), `snake_traversal.gd` (`zip_target` + one line in `_preview`, three lines at the top of `button_label`), `ui/touch_controls.gd` (the `SnakeFx.hud_cues` line before the sprites are drawn).
- Open: a roof zip still covers 25 to 40 m in 0.3 to 0.4 s (flight log defect 3: reads as a teleport); one roof zip of ten in the w116 probe ended on the street; the cues exist on the touch HUD only (a desktop gets the old toasts plus the energy toast); the marker reads the anchor under the crosshair only (no marker for the swing anchor on the ground).

## Wave 117 critic (agent w117-critic: an independent finger-only review of the swing, the rope and the zip, then fixes; log docs/sprint/progress/w117-critic.md)
- How it was measured: `godot/tests/dev_w117_critic.gd` (probe, real fingers, a recorder per physics tick; `CR_PARTS=hold0,hold1,hold2,press0,early,relift,jump,wall,noanchor,ziproof,zipwall,fly,ground,scan`) and `godot/tests/bot_w117_critic.gd` (frame strips at 1200x540 LOW, `CRB_SCENE=hold|jump|wall|ziproof|zipwall|fly|noanchor`; render ONE at a time, two at once ran out of memory). Three streets: (-30, 0, 4) looking +X and -X, (240, 0, -305) looking -Z.
- Fixed, each with a switch that restores the builders' state and a check in `godot/tests/test_critic_w117.gd` (17 checks PASS; `CRITIC_OLD=1`: 9 FAIL):
  1. A held chain climbed about 10 m a swing (34 to 47 m up after 12 s on all three streets, ropes of 7 to 10 m at the roofline, free falls of 1.5 to 2 s). Now it levels off near 20 m: a rope the held chain fired lets go flatter the higher he is (`SwingMotion.rel_front`, 0.62 rad under 9 m to 0.2 rad at 20 m) and the anchor score likes ropes whose lowest point is back at cruise height (`SwingMotion.scan_h`, set by `SnakeAnchors.find_swing`; off while he falls faster than 14 m/s, where the rope that catches him counts). Switch `SwingMotion.level_chain`. A swing the player pressed for keeps its whole arc (`SnakeSwing.fresh_press`).
  2. JUMP out of a swing also fired the QUANTUM GLIDE burst one tick later (14.6 to 27.1 m/s along the camera, the swing's velocity gone): a JUMP a snake state took stays taken until the thumb lifts (`SnakeTraversal._jump_taken`, switch `jump_takes`).
  3. A roof ZIP peaked at 101 m/s (1.69 m in a tick): `SnakeZip.peak_speed` 44 m/s lengthens the pull (31 m: 1.5 s), at most 2.2 s (zips over 46 m are faster again: test_flight_cost_w116 gives a zip 3 s).
  4. The rope of a roof zip bit the WALL under the crosshair and hung down from the hand while he rose: it bites the roof's edge (`SnakeZip.rope_to_edge`).
  5. He arrived flat, then head down over the roof (`SnakePose.zip_upright`, pull phase only).
  6. In a held chain a thumb lifted within 0.25 s of an auto-fired bite was read as a tap and let go a second later (`SwingMotion.tap_needs_press`).
  7. Between two ropes he stood upright in the air: a thrown pose that leans along the velocity (`SnakePose.throw_pose`, `SnakeTraversal.thrown()`).
  8. The chain's arc came within 1.2 to 1.6 m of the street after the long falls: gone with 1 (check: at least 2 m of air on every rope of the three chains). A separate pull-up was written and removed: no constructed fall reproduced the sag.
- Open (seen, not fixed): a wall ZIP aimed low hauls to the roof however high (74 and 81 m measured, 3.8 s); SNAKE on the street is ZIP (a swing needs JUMP first); FLY is not on screen on the ground; the levelled chain dips only 3 to 5 m a swing at 15 to 20 m (level, not deep dives: the fan has 6 pitch rows and the rope takes up slack); test_swing_w117's wall part is flaky under CPU load (see the log); test_critic_look_w115 "the trial reproduces the old fling" was red before this review.

## Wave 118 armour: tanks, helicopters and planes the player can find (builder w118-armour; log docs/sprint/progress/w118-armour.md; architecture section "Wave 118 armour" of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`)
- Owner: "where are tanks and helicopters etc. everything is pending for such long time". Found by finger play (probe `godot/tests/dev_w118_armour.gd`): the nearest fixed vehicle was 137 m from the start and only existed within 100 m; the five tanks stood in the traffic lanes of the west ring road 300 m out; their map markers were fogged GARAGE car glyphs (a fresh player saw none); on LOW+ (2 live cars) each new tank released the one beside it; a tank on a kerb lot was stopped after 11 m by a bus shelter and bollards, and kerb cars parked across the way out.
- What there is now: the ARMY YARD (plaza (-135, -55), 120 m from the start: battle tank, light tank, armoured carrier, nose to the road, paint, barriers, sign), CITY HELIPAD EAST (105, 25: helicopter) and SOUTH (-25, 185: gunship), the airport's helicopters and planes kept like them (`ArmourSites.FIXED_AIRPORT`). All in `godot/scripts/vehicles/armour_sites.gd`. Fixed vehicles stand outside the car cap, appear 112 m out and are kept while the hero is within 150 m.
- Map: chip TANKS + AIR, tank / helicopter / plane icons that are never fogged, an edge pin for a kind that is off the view (tap = GPS), glyphs on the minimap. Fixed on the way: a legend chip of the always-kinds (SITES, HOME, MISSION, SIDE and the new one) vanished with its own tap when it was switched off and could never be switched on again (`MapScreen._chip_shown`).
- CALL-IN: MORE > CALL opens a list (CAR, TANK, HELICOPTER, PLANE, CLOSE; `godot/scripts/ui/call_in.gd`); cooldown 90 / 60 / 60 s. DEMO and TEST menu: the SPAWN tab starts with TANKS, HELICOPTERS, PLANES: NEXT TO YOU.
- Check: `godot/tests/test_armour_w118.gd` (43 checks, real fingers: map icons and GPS, the demo list, walk to the tank in the yard, ENTER, drive 30 m, FIRE, EXIT; the helicopter: ENTER, UP, fly, FIRE, DOWN, EXIT; the call-in). `ARMOUR_OLD=1`: 12 checks fail. Frames `docs/shots/round7/armour_w118/armour_w118_low.jpg` (bot `godot/tests/bot_w118_armour.gd`).
- ENTER beside a delivery (restart 2, seen in the frame `call_2_tank`: the prompt read HIJACK SEDAN next to the delivered tank, and w118-controls saw ENTER board a traffic car beside a spawned plane): `VehicleManager.reach_car` now prefers a vehicle with the meta `called` or `fixed` over other vehicles in reach (score -3 m; switch `ArmourSites.pick_on`). Check in part `call` of the test (`ARMOUR_PICK_OLD=1 ARMOUR_PARTS=call`: it fails, the hero ends in the sedan).
- TEST NOTE: `TestLib.frames` waits PHYSICS ticks. On a loaded machine several ticks fit in one engine frame and the input buffer is flushed once a frame, so a finger lift sent with `Input.parse_input_event` arrives AFTER the check that reads it (200-tap probe: 0 late lifts alone, 21 to 35 of 200 with three tests running). After a parsed touch wait `physics_frame` then `process_frame`. `tests/test_touch_buttons_r7.gd` was flaky for this reason (its `_tap` now waits whole frames); other finger tests that use `TestLib.frames` after a tap can flake the same way under load.
- Open: the small plane of the city still stands on the avenue at (340, 0, 0) in traffic (no airstrip of its own); a called-in vehicle can land beside moving traffic; the yard has no gate guard, no enemies and no mission; planes are not in the finger test (take-off needs the long avenue); the CALL button still reads CALL CAR (the label belongs to w118-controls).

## Wave 118 controls (agent w118-controls: GTA-style on-screen controls, one small set of round buttons per mode; log docs/sprint/progress/w118-controls.md; architecture section "Wave 118 controls" of `godot/ARCHITECTURE.md`)
- Owner (2026-11-21): "make sure all controls come as options on screen like gta. It should not be cluttered but like 5 circle for driving etc." What it is now (`godot/scripts/ui/touch_controls.gd`, table `BUTTONS`): every action of the mode the hero is in is a round button, each mode shows only its own set, the stick is the left thumb, the way out of a mode (ENTER / EXIT / LAND / LEAVE) is ONE slot in every mode. ON FOOT: FIRE (big), AIM, JUMP, SWING, ZIP, FLY, ENTER beside a vehicle; small CROUCH, RELOAD, ABILITY above the stick; WEAPON, MORE, PAUSE at the top. SNAKE FLIGHT: FIRE, AIM, UP, DOWN, BOOST, LAND. CAR / BIKE / BOAT: GAS (big), BRAKE (then reverse), HAND BRAKE, EXIT, FIRE only on an armed car; small HORN, VIEW. TANK: FIRE, second gun, BRAKE, EXIT; small VIEW, SHELL; the stick drives, a drag on the open screen turns the turret. HELICOPTER / PLANE: FIRE, ROCKET / MISSILE, UP, DOWN, EXIT, VIEW. INDOORS: FIRE, AIM, JUMP, USE (only at a spot that offers something), LEAVE. MORE holds only extras (MELEE, THROW, ALT, GADGET, CALL, AMMO, C4, ULT, ally orders; RADIO, GARAGE, BACK while driving), as two short rows under the top-right row.

The wave 116 audit is extended (`tests/touch_audit_lib.gd` checks the drawn 56 / 44 px, `tests/test_touch_audit.gd` asserts that every play HUD frame has no geometry finding and the new buttons). Pictures: `godot/tests/bot_controls_w118.gd`, `docs/shots/round7/controls_w118/` (one frame per mode at 844x390 and 2400x1080, `sheet_844.jpg`).
- Old tests changed because the layout changed (each has a "wave 118" comment): `test_hud_declutter` and `test_world_hud` MAX_CONTROLS 13 -> 16 (ZIP, FLY and CROUCH are buttons now: the owner's request against the older cap; an on-foot frame is 11 discs + PAUSE, was 8 + PAUSE); `test_hud_camera_final`, `test_director_fixes_b`, `test_director_round2_a/c`, `test_companion`, `test_radio_ui`, `test_vehicle_realism`, `test_menu_cut`, `test_snake_combat` (layout by `TouchControls.NEED_MODES` / `exclusive` / `centre_at` instead of their own tables); `test_snake_traversal`, `test_rope_w117`, `test_critic_w117`, `test_flight_cost_w116`, `test_flight_view_w116` (a zip is pressed on ZIP; their OLD trials run the one-button layout); `tests/interior_bot.gd` (plays on a 16:9 window: on the square headless window the left row folds over the right cluster and its stick touch landed on GRAB).

## Wave 119 HUD: five circles on foot and the wave 118 leftovers (builder w119-hud5; log docs/sprint/progress/w119-hud5.md)
- On foot the right cluster is exactly 5 round buttons: FIRE, JUMP, SWING, ZIP, and the context slot (ENTER / USE / ride word in reach, else SNAKE BLAST, action `snake_blast`, label by w119-blasts). Small edge chips (rounded plates, `"chip": true` in `TouchControls.BUTTONS`): CROUCH, RELOAD, ABILITY, FLY. WEAPON (tap swap / hold wheel), MORE, PAUSE stay on top. AIM on the street is auto-aim (AimAssist while FIRE is held); the AIM circle stays in snake flight and indoors. One SNAKE button for swing + zip + fly was dropped: the hold IS the rope (a held swing chains), so a hold cannot also be FLY, and tap-zip vs tap-swing near an anchor is the wave 117 bug. A held SWING with nothing to bite still flies.

## Wave 119 CI fixes (CI run 45070818207 on cae4b52: shards red; watcher w119-ci-apk, log docs/sprint/progress/w119-ci-apk.md)
- `test_critic_w118` "a finger on ENTER: in the tank" (both attempts; reproduced 2 of 4 runs here). Two test set-up races with wave 119 behaviour, no game bug: (1) the w119 critic made the outdoor context slot turn from SNAKE BLAST to ENTER only after a vehicle has been in reach `TouchControls.SLOT_SETTLE` (0.25 s); the test pressed after 20 frames (less than 0.25 s at 140 fps), `Fingers.press` found no ENTER button and sent nothing (probe: `slot=false slot_t=0.20`). (2) A police interceptor tailing the hero was in reach and `reach_car` boarded it instead of the test tank. Fix (test only): `_wait_enter()` waits (max 3 s) until the slot reads ENTER before the finger goes down (heli and tank), and the tank wears the `spawned` meta of the demo SPAWN list, which `reach_car` already prefers. 4 of 4 runs pass after.
- `test_vehicles_city` "parked cars stand on the parking bays" (both attempts; reproduced 1 of 3 runs here, also with `W119C_OLD=1`, so not the critic). The off-bay "parked" car was always a siren car (ambulance, interceptor): a traffic car that lost its lane in a crash (`Vehicle.lane_lost`, `ai=false`) stands where it hit and was counted as a kerb car (ambulances and patrol cars never park, `ParkedCars.NO_KINDS`). Fix: `Vehicle.lost_lane` (set by `lane_lost`, cleared on activate and `set_traffic`); the test skips such cars and prints them ("interceptor lost its lane in a crash at ...", seen in the verifying run), every real kerb car is still checked.
- `test_hud_declutter` "a parked car can be placed next to the hero" and `test_critic_look_w115` "a parked sedan stands beside the hero" (shard 0, both attempts). Both pass alone; REPRODUCED by replaying the first 24 tests of shard 0 in order with one shared `XDG_DATA_HOME`, and bisected to ONE file: `roam_world.cfg` with `rides=2` written by an earlier test. With it the two discovery rides wait on their pads by the spawn (`RoamData.ride_points`), the tier's car cap is full and `spawn_parked` returns null. Cause: `tools/ci_shard.sh` ran every test of a shard with the same user://, so a save left behind by one test changed the next (the wave 119 test files moved the shard order). Fix: each attempt in `ci_shard.sh` gets its own empty `XDG_DATA_HOME` (mktemp, removed after), as the integrators' local full-suite wrapper already does.
- FLAKY on the runner, passed on the retry, not touched: `test_shooting_accuracy` (99.4 %, 177 / 178), `test_w119_critic` "the weapon wheel opens to reach the BOMBS wedge", `test_interior_tasks_ui` stealth copy (known).
- Round 2 (CI 45077691498 on f0d1f71): every shard but 3 green; shard 3 red on `test_w119_critic` "the bomb is aimed at the street under the crosshair, not at a point in the air ([(-30.57, 1.61, 2.95)])" (both attempts; in the first run this test was only FLAKY on another check). The aim point stood 0.6 m in front of the hero at chest height: the crosshair ray (world, vehicle and enemy layers) hit something right in front of him, so `SnakeBombs.throw` rightly aimed at it (the sky-to-street projection only applies when the ray hits nothing). NOT reproduced here (3 of 3 pass with a fresh user://, the ray hits nothing and the aim lands at x -63.8 on the street). The test's premise is an empty ray, so the step now clears the foes (a snake hunter the earlier casts may have called) and the cars before it turns the crosshair, and the check prints what the ray hit before the throw and where the hero stood, so a recurrence names its collider. Not a game change. FLAKY on the runner, passed on the retry: `test_superhero_hero` (female floor, known), `test_mission_board` stuck findings, `test_melee_gadgets` anchor pull, `test_touch_audit` garage dead button, `test_damage_everywhere` fire spread. Also seen here under 4 parallel copies and not touched: `test_w119_critic` "a car that comes and goes every 4 frames does not flip the slot" (1 to 3 flips; `_use_near` is sampled every 0.15 s, so the 0.25 s settle sees two or three samples: open for the w119 critic owner).

## Wave 122 CI fixes (CI run 45587916456 on 7c4fe7e: shard 0 red; watcher w122-ci-apk)
- `test_mission_audit` failed on both attempts with ONE finding: GUIDE_RECOVERED in chapter 2 (the courier chase / the kill steps after it). `test_soak_quick` was FLAKY (node-count leak check at checkpoint 1, passed on the retry; not touched: a probe opening and closing 40 shops grew no nodes).
- REPRODUCED with the DejaVu fonts.conf: the full audit failed 3 of 3 here on HEAD and passed 4 of 4 with `W122_SHOPS_OLD=1`. Cause: the wave 122 walk-in shops (`ShopFronts`) opened and closed while the hero DROVE past them: each open swaps the lot's collider boxes, builds the room, the facade copy and the shopkeeper (4 to 92 ms measured), shop after shop along the chase route, under the car.
- Fix (`scripts/world/shop_fronts.gd`): no shop opens while the hero is in a vehicle (he walks into a shop; a car cannot enter the glass front anyway). After the fix the full audit passed 4 of 5 local runs; the one failure was the long-known chase flake (TARGET ESCAPED three times, then the guide's recovery). The shop tests (`test_shops_w122`, `test_shop_look_w122c`, `test_doors_w122c`) pass.
- Round 2 (CI 45601897586 on 2a7f90f): shard 0 green. `test_w119_critic` "sp_frost ... the tank takes damage (5000 -> 4980)": FROST deals exactly its table damage to a car (20); the check (more than 20) had passed only because the BLAZE throw before it left a 6 s ground fire at the same spot that kept burning the next tank (`XDMG` probe on `Vehicle.damage`: 4.5 a tick from `blast_ops.gd` burn). Fix (test, meaning kept: each species alone must hurt the tank by its own damage): `BlastOps.reset()` before each species and the bound `<= h0 - 20`; reproduced 4980 here, passes. `test_controls_w118` (ENTER beside a plane boarded the sedan, both attempts on the runner) passed here; watched in the next run.
- Round 3 (CI 45609085794 on 26662ab): shard 2 red on `test_integration` "the composed game stays small: 3005 nodes" (limit 3000) on both attempts. Breakdown per Main child (probe in a copy of the test): wave 121 2975 nodes, wave 122 about 3000 to 3020: ShopFronts +1, RoofParapets +1, Mirror +2, and 9 idle nodes of the TowerFloors people pool built in `_ready` (3 InteriorCivilians), although `_place_actors` builds the pool at the first tower entry anyway; the remaining spread is the enemy pool at that moment (EnemyManager 58 to 97 nodes). Fix: the eager pool is gone (made at the first entry and kept); 2990 and 2994 here. Note for whoever owns the budget: the margin under 3000 is now about the size of the enemy pool's run-to-run spread.
